# Why this exists

The original plan at `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan/plans/build-the-higher-abstraction-20260425-0953/final.md` was a full 20-task brief that landed about 4 tasks (T1, T2, T5, T10) before its execute step returned `partial` twice and the plan stalled at `state=aborted`. Subsequent work (`design-a-concise-architecture-20260426-1555` → `p1-foundation-typed-handle-20260426-1927`) shipped a typed `Handle` dataclass and `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs).out(...)` authoring API, but did NOT cover the higher-abstraction layer (ops, artifacts, router, recipes, plugin discovery). So the framework exists but the user-facing "ordinary Python pipelines" surface does not.

The original plan doc is the source of truth for design decisions and watch items — read it before planning. This brief deliberately does not restate task details that are still accurate there. Where the original plan's design has been improved by p1-foundation (typed Handle, `wf.node().out()`), prefer the newer API.

# What is already shipped (DO NOT REDO)

Verified by inspection on 2026-04-27:

- `vibecomfy/blocks/` — `__init__.py`, `_utils.py`, `decode.py`, `encoding.py`, `latent.py`, `loaders.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `subgraph.py`, `video.py`. The `Handles` wrapper exists and is typed against the new `Handle` dataclass per p1-foundation.
- `vibecomfy/patches/` — `builtins.py`, `controlnet.py`, `gguf_unet.py`, `ltx_lowvram.py`, `requirements.py`, `resolution.py`, `save_prefix.py`, `seed.py`, `registry.py`, `types.py`. Original T4 patch list (gguf_unet, ltx_lowvram, resolution, seed, save_prefix) plus extras.
- `VibeWorkflow.finalize_metadata()` — `vibecomfy/workflow.py:97`. Helpers in `vibecomfy/metadata.py`.
- `Handle` dataclass + `wf.node(class_type).out(slot)` typed authoring API — `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:16`, `vibecomfy/workflow.py:129`. Use these, not the older "string-only refs" assumption from the original plan.
- `tests/snapshots/` for 8 templates — pre-refactor API + class_type + widget_value snapshots already captured. Ready to drive snapshot-diff tests.
- `docs/authoring.md` (125 lines) — block + patch contracts, finalize_metadata, subgraph.opaque, "changes-handles → new template, decorates-handles → patch" rule. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 (ops/artifacts/router/recipes/plugin discovery) — see C5 below.**
- `AGENTS.md` at repo root. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 — see C5 below.**
- `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` exports `run_embedded`, `run_embedded_sync`, `smoke_runtime`, `smoke_runtime_sync` (T10 done).
- `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` includes the `untyped_raw_ref` lint rule from p1-foundation (separate from T17 patch suggestions, which is still missing).

## Layer A — Typed surface (was original T11–T13)

A1. **`vibecomfy/artifacts.py`** — frozen `Artifact` dataclass (`workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`. Methods: `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow`, `compile() -> dict`, `run(*, runtime='embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.{run_embedded_sync, run_sync}`.

   Note: original T10 listed this as "Depends on T1" and was marked done in `final.md`, but `vibecomfy/artifacts.py` does not exist on disk — verify and ship if missing. (Likely got rolled back when the plan aborted.)

A2. **`vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py` + `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`** — verb-native API returning typed Artifacts, lazy by default. Signatures from original T11:
   - `image.t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `image.edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `video.t2v(prompt, *, model=None, width=None, height=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `video.i2v(image, prompt, *, model=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `audio.t2a(...)` raises `NotImplementedError('no audio template registered')`
   - `edit.qwen(image, instruction, **overrides) -> Image` delegates to `image.edit`

   Op body: pick `(template_id, [patch, ...])` via the router (A3); load via `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)` (B1) or `workflow_from_template`; build VibeWorkflow; apply `set_prompt`/`set_seed` and patches; return typed artifact pointing at the workflow's terminal save node from `outputs`.

A3. **`vibecomfy/router.py` + `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`** — hand-written decision table per original T12. Inputs: `verb`, `inputs` (image presence, requested model, requested resolution/length), `environment` (declared model presence + GPU memory hint, both pluggable; for v1 stub `environment` to `{}` and document that callers can override — exhaustive GPU detection is MP-5 territory).

   **Rules must be exhaustive across all 9 refactored templates** (see C1) — every template must be reachable via at least one rule. The full required rule set covering the 9 templates:
   - `('image', 't2i', model in {'z_image', None}) → 'image/z_image'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf') → 'image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_t2v'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_t2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_i2v'`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_i2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('image', 'edit', model in {'qwen', None}) → 'edit/qwen_image_edit'`
   - `('image', 'edit', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled'`
   - Fall back to `KeyError('no template for verb=...')`. After template selection, call `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)` so router and doctor (C2) share the same probe. Data-driven rules in `router_rules.py` so plugins can extend via `register_route(verb, predicate, template_id, patches=())`.

A4. **Tests**: `tests/test_router.py` (rule table positive/negative + KeyError); `tests/test_ops.py` (`image.t2i('hello')` returns Image without executing; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` has SaveImage and a node carrying the prompt; `preview_workflow().inputs['prompt']` registered — proves finalize_metadata wires through ops). # Critical constraints (from original plan + new context)

- **VibeWorkflow remains the only editable IR.** Blocks must mutate via `add_node`/`connect`; patches must return VibeWorkflow; only `ingest/normalize.py` and `compile()` may edit API dicts directly.
- **Backward compatibility:** existing tests pass unchanged. Existing CLI flag surfaces stay identical. `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public-facing imports/CLI args must not diff.
- **MarkdownNote drop vs snapshot parity:** snapshots in `tests/snapshots/` were captured with MarkdownNote filtered at the source. Refactored templates drop MarkdownNote; snapshot test compares only runnable nodes. If you re-capture without filtering, parity test fails and refactor looks broken.
- **finalize_metadata parity is load-bearing:** `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` must show `inputs/outputs/requirements` equal between block-built workflow + `finalize_metadata()` and `convert_to_vibe_format()` for an equivalent graph. If parity breaks, `set_prompt`/`set_seed`/inspect/op-terminal-artifact-lookup all silently degrade.
- **ltx_lowvram external_python_marker parity:** new patch must produce the same marker key/value as the legacy `_ready_template_policy` mutator before that function is deleted. Verify with `tests/test_ready_templates.py:19` still green.
- **`vibecomfy/cli.py:23 _resolve_workflow_path`** must be either deleted or made to delegate to `load_workflow_any` — duplicate logic in two places will diverge.
- **Plugin discovery cold-start cost:** `_resolve_ready_path` calls `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily; cache empty-plugin result so `workflows list` does not slow on every invocation.
- **Built-in ready-template ids win on duplicate id with plugin templates;** collisions emit a warning, not an exception.
- **Lazy ops contract:** verbs return Artifact objects without executing; `Artifact.run()` triggers execution; `Artifact.preview_workflow()` returns the VibeWorkflow without running. Pipelines compose via file-handoff — single-workflow chaining is out of scope for v1.
- **`audio.t2a` is a `NotImplementedError` stub** until an audio ready template ships. Document in AGENTS.md and `docs/authoring.md`.
- **Each block/patch/op file should stay under ~250 lines** with a single responsibility.
- **`subgraph.opaque` is a passthrough wrapper for UUID-class subgraph nodes;** it cannot be decomposed without unpacking the ComfyUI subgraph definition. Each affected template names the output slot it actually wires (typically slot 0 → 'out').
- **Repo is NOT a git repo.** Do NOT run `git init`. Verify `.git/` does not exist after each phase.

# Out of scope (do NOT do)

- Re-doing anything in the "already shipped" list — verify it still works, but don't reimplement.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` external-mode handling / `ExternalServerRestartRequired` — deferred to MP-5 by p1-foundation.
- Block library expansion beyond what's needed for the 8-template refactor.
- VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration, `wrap_api_dict`, ExternalPythonNode, stage-aware run-dirs / `flow_id` / `stage_index` — MP-5 territory per the design doc.
- Schema-backed validation, `/object_info` snapshot, `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` — MP-6 territory.
- Compile-time serialization gate — MP-3 territory.
- RunPod end-to-end smoke as part of acceptance — `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` exists from p1-foundation but stays opt-in (`@pytest.mark.runpod`); do NOT execute during megaplan run.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Finish Higher-Abstraction Authoring Layer

## Overview

The Layer 1 foundation (`blocks/`, `patches/`, `Handle`, `wf.node().out()`, `finalize_metadata`, snapshots, `docs/authoring.md`) is shipped. This plan adds the **user-facing Layer 2**: typed `Artifact`, verb-native ops (`image.t2i / video.t2v / image.edit`), a hand-written `router`, plugin discovery (`ensure_plugins_loaded`), unified CLI loader (`load_workflow_any`), `--json` output across the listed commands, doctor patch suggestions, refactor of the 9 snapshotted ready-templates onto blocks, three example recipes, and an `[entry-points]` declaration. Behavior of the other ~40 templates and existing CLI surfaces stays unchanged.

The brief is well-researched and verified largely correct against the repo. Confirmed missing on disk: `vibecomfy/{artifacts.py, ops/, router.py, router_rules.py, cli_loader.py, extras.py}`, `recipes/`, plugin entry-point group, doctor patch suggestions, `--json` on the listed commands, and the 9 block-based template builders. Confirmed shipped: blocks, patches, runtime exports, untyped_raw_ref lint, `Handle`, `wf.node().out()`, snapshots, authoring doc.

A few brief inaccuracies that don't change the plan: (a) `_resolve_workflow_path` actually lives at `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py:19` (named `resolve_workflow_path`); (b) the `external_python_marker` assertion is at `tests/test_ready_templates.py:103`, not `:19`; (c) `.git/` does in fact exist (current branch `codex/composition-ready-templates`) — I will leave it alone (see Question 1).

---

## Phase 1: Typed Surface (Layer A)

### Step 1: Add `vibecomfy/artifacts.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** the frozen `Artifact` dataclass (`workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask` (each just sets the `kind` default in `__init__`/factory; subclasses are tag types, not behavior overrides).
2. **Implement** methods: `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow` (returns `self.workflow`); `compile() -> dict` (returns `self.workflow.compile('api')`); `run(*, runtime: str = 'embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded_sync` for `'embedded'` and `vibecomfy.runtime.run_sync` for `'server'` (raise `ValueError` on unknown runtime).
3. **Keep** the file under ~100 lines, no extra abstractions.

### Step 2: Add the router (`vibecomfy/router.py`, `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `router_rules.py`: a list of `Rule(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches_factory)` plus `register_route(verb, predicate, template_id, patches=())` to append rules. Seed with the 9 rules from the brief verbatim. The LTX rules return `(template_id, [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])` — the LTX patch is already singleton (`vibecomfy.patches.ltx_lowvram.patch`), so wrap it as `lambda: vibecomfy.patches.ltx_lowvram.patch` and `lambda: vibecomfy.patches.resolution(384, 256, 9)`.
2. **Create** `router.py`: `pick(verb_kind: str, verb_name: str, **inputs) -> tuple[str, list[Patch]]`. Walk rules in registration order, run predicate on `(inputs)`, return on first match. After resolving the template_id, lazily call `find_applicable(workflow)` only when the caller asks (the brief says "router and doctor share the same probe" — implement this as a separate helper `applicable_patches(workflow) -> list[Patch]` re-exported from `vibecomfy.router` so doctor and ops both call it).
3. **Raise** `KeyError(f'no template for verb={verb_kind!r}/{verb_name!r}')` on miss.
4. **Predicates** are small lambdas reading `inputs` dict (`lambda i: i.get('model') in {'z_image', None}`).

### Step 3: Add `vibecomfy/ops/` package
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py` re-exporting submodule namespaces: `from vibecomfy.ops import image, video, audio, edit`.
2. **Create** `vibecomfy/ops/image.py` with `t2i` and `edit`. Body: `template_id, patches = router.pick('image', 't2i', model=model, ...)`, load via `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)`, `wf.set_prompt(prompt)`, `wf.set_seed(seed)` if seed is not None, apply each patch via `patch.apply(wf)`, then locate the terminal save node from `wf.outputs` (first `VibeOutput` whose `output_type` is `SaveImage`), return `Image(workflow=wf, node_id=output.node_id, output_slot=0, kind='image')`.
3. **Create** `vibecomfy/ops/video.py` with `t2v` and `i2v` (same pattern, terminal `SaveVideo` → `Video`).
4. **Create** `vibecomfy/ops/audio.py` with `t2a` raising `NotImplementedError("no audio template registered")`.
5. **Create** `vibecomfy/ops/edit.py` with `qwen(image, instruction, **overrides) -> Image` delegating to `image.edit`.
6. **Resolution / steps / fps overrides** apply via the parameterised `vibecomfy.patches.resolution(...)` patch when the caller passes width/height/length; treat `steps` and `fps` as widget overrides (use `wf.set_steps()` if available; fps writes `widget_0` on the `CreateVideo` node — keep it minimal and document in C5).

### Step 4: Router + ops tests (`tests/test_router.py`, `tests/test_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `tests/test_router.py` covering each of the 9 rules positively (returns expected `template_id` + patch names) plus a negative `KeyError`.
2. **Create** `tests/test_ops.py`: `image.t2i('hello')` returns an `Image`; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` contains a `SaveImage` node and the prompt registered via `wf.inputs['prompt']` (proves finalize_metadata wires through ops). Skip end-to-end runs (no `Artifact.run()` call in CI).

## Phase 2: CLI Unification + Plugin Discovery (Layer B)

### Step 5: Unified loader (`vibecomfy/cli_loader.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `load_workflow_any(path_or_id: str) -> VibeWorkflow` with the four-step dispatch from the brief: ready-template id (via `ready_template_ids()`) → `workflow_from_ready`; existing `.py` file → `load_scratchpad`; existing `.json` file → `convert_to_vibe_format(normalize_to_api(load_template(path), schema_provider=get_schema_provider('auto')), source_path=path, schema_provider=get_schema_provider('auto'))`; fall back to existing index lookup via `resolve_workflow_path` then re-dispatch.
2. **Add** `tests/test_cli_loader.py` covering ready-template id, slash-separated id, scratchpad path, JSON path, and missing-id `KeyError`.

### Step 6: Refactor commands to use `load_workflow_any` (`vibecomfy/commands/{doctor,inspect,run,validate,convert}.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** the path-resolution logic in `doctor.py`, `inspect.py`, `run.py`, `validate.py`, `convert.py` with calls to `load_workflow_any` (or a thin shim that calls it then unwraps to a path when the command needs the file path, e.g. `convert`). The existing `vibecomfy.registry.load_workflow_reference` keeps working — replace only the path-resolution arm, not registry lookups already covered.
2. **Make** `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py:resolve_workflow_path` either delegate to `load_workflow_any` (for callers that still need just a path string — e.g. `doctor._json_path_for_reference`) or stay scoped to JSON-index lookup with a docstring noting it is the lower layer. The brief explicitly forbids duplicate logic — pick "delegate" so there is one source of truth.
3. **Run** `pytest tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py` after this step to catch regressions.

### Step 7: Plugin discovery (`vibecomfy/extras.py`, `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/extras.py` with `PluginAPI` (a small dataclass exposing `register_block`, `register_patch`, `register_op`, `register_route`, `register_ready_root`), `_DISCOVERED: dict | None = None` cache, `load_plugins() -> PluginAPI` doing the three sources, and `ensure_plugins_loaded()` calling `load_plugins()` once and caching even an empty result.
2. **Implement** the three discovery sources: glob `./vibecomfy_extras/{blocks,patches,ops,recipes,ready_templates}/*.py` (importlib spec_from_file_location); glob `~/.vibecomfy/{...}/*.py`; `importlib.metadata.entry_points(group='vibecomfy.plugins')` calling each `ep.load()(api)`.
3. **Refactor** `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`: replace module-level `READY_ROOT` with `_ready_roots() -> list[Path]` returning built-in repo dir, `./vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates`, `~/.vibecomfy/ready_templates`, plus `register_ready_root` paths. `ready_template_ids()` walks all roots, builds a dict keyed by id, built-in winning on duplicate ids (warn via `warnings.warn` once per collision); `_resolve_ready_path` walks all roots in order. Both call `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily.
4. **Expose** `ensure_plugins_loaded` from the package root (`vibecomfy/__init__.py`).
5. **Add** `tests/test_plugin_discovery.py` with three cases: a project-local fixture under `tmp_path/vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/`, a user-global fixture using `tmp_path` + `monkeypatch.setenv('HOME', tmp_path)` (or monkeypatch `pathlib.Path.home`), an entry-point fixture using `importlib.metadata` mocking. Each registers one block, patch, op, route, and ready-template root; assertions cover discovery + collision warning.

### Step 8: `--json` output across listed commands (`vibecomfy/commands/_output.py`, plus the listed command modules)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/commands/_output.py` with `emit(rows_or_obj, *, json: bool, columns: list[str] | None = None)` — JSON path uses `json.dumps(..., default=str, indent=2)`; default tab-separated path keeps the existing format unchanged.
2. **Add** `--json` flag to `workflows list`, `nodes list`, `inspect`, `doctor`, `sources sync` (summary), `analyze info`, `analyze diff`. For commands that already have `--json` (verified in `nodes`, `analyze`, `runpod`), route through `_output.emit` for consistency.
3. **Inspect `--json` payload** must include `applicable_patches: [{"name": ..., "rationale": ...}]` from `vibecomfy.router.applicable_patches(workflow)` (which delegates to `find_applicable`).
4. **All `--json` paths use `load_workflow_any`** (already covered in Step 6 for the ones that load workflows).

### Step 9: Public exports + smoke (`vibecomfy/__init__.py`, `pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `vibecomfy/__init__.py` to export: `Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any` (plus everything already exported). Keep alphabetical-ish order; add to `__all__`.
2. **Declare** the plugin entry-point group in `pyproject.toml`:
   ```toml
   [project.entry-points."vibecomfy.plugins"]
   # External packages register plugins here:
   #   my_plugin = "my_pkg:register"
   ```
3. **Smoke** with `python -m vibecomfy.cli {sources,workflows,nodes,analyze,search,inspect,convert,validate,doctor,fetch,models,run,runtime,session,logs,runpod,watchdog} --help`. Expand the help check to all 17 registered commands (the brief listed 14, but `fetch`, `models`, `session`, `watchdog` are also registered — see Assumption 2).

## Phase 3: Doctor + Ready-Template Refactor + Recipes (Layer C)

### Step 10: Doctor patch suggestions (`vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **After** computing `report` in `_cmd_doctor`, call `vibecomfy.router.applicable_patches(workflow)` and emit `Suggested patches: <name>: <rationale>` lines (or `suggested_patches` JSON field when `--json` is set per Step 8).
2. **Verify** `controlnet`, `gguf_unet`, `ltx_lowvram` already define `rationale(workflow) -> str` (confirmed in `vibecomfy/patches/ltx_lowvram.py:56`). For new `resolution(...)` patch instances created at suggestion time we don't suggest parametric patches automatically — only registered builtins from `find_applicable` are surfaced.

### Step 11: Refactor 9 ready_templates onto blocks (`ready_templates/{video,image,edit}/<id>.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Verify the LTX `external_python_marker`** parity first: build the current `video/ltx2_3_t2v` via the existing path, build a hand-written equivalent through blocks + apply `ltx_lowvram` patch, diff `metadata['external_python_marker']` and `comfy_configuration`. Keep `vibecomfy/patches/ltx_lowvram.py` untouched if marker matches; if not, fix the patch first before deleting `_ready_template_policy`.
2. **Refactor each of the 9 templates one at a time** in this order, running its snapshot test after each:
   - `image/z_image.py` (smallest, 27-line snapshot — uses `subgraph.opaque` for UUID class type)
   - `image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py`, `image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py`
   - `edit/qwen_image_edit.py`, `edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled.py`
   - `video/wan_t2v.py`, `video/wan_i2v.py`
   - `video/ltx2_3_t2v.py`, `video/ltx2_3_i2v.py`
3. **Each refactored builder** uses blocks (`unet_clip_vae`, `text_pair`, `ksampler`, `decode`, `video.create`, `save.image`/`save.video`) or `subgraph.opaque` for UUID class types, and ends with `wf.finalize_metadata()`. Use the typed `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs).out(...)` API where blocks don't fit. Drop inline `API_WORKFLOW`, `MarkdownNote` nodes, and the call to `build_api_ready_workflow`. The builder must still set `metadata.update(READY_METADATA)` and call a small helper to merge `READY_REQUIREMENTS` (extract `apply_ready_template_policy`-style metadata-merge into `vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py:apply_authored_metadata(workflow, metadata, requirements)` so refactored builders share it).
4. **For the LTX templates**, the builder applies `ltx_lowvram.apply(wf)` and `resolution(384, 256, 9).apply(wf)` inline (or via a small `_apply_ltx_smoke_policy(wf)` helper) so `compile()` matches the snapshot without needing the router.
5. **Snapshot diff test** in `tests/test_ready_templates.py`: add `test_refactored_template_matches_snapshot` parametrized over the 9 ids that loads `tests/snapshots/<id>.api.json`, builds via `workflow_from_ready`, compares `compile('api')` with node-id reordering allowed (compare sets of `(class_type, sorted_widget_value_items)` plus link topology) and MarkdownNote filtered out of the snapshot. Do **not** re-capture snapshots.
6. **Keep** existing assertions in `test_all_ready_templates_load_and_validate` (loads ALL 49 templates) green — verifies the unrefactored ~40 still work via `build_api_ready_workflow`.
7. **`metadata['python_policy_applied'] = True`**: existing test `test_ready_template_loads_vibe_workflow` at `tests/test_ready_templates.py:25` asserts this on `edit/qwen_image_edit`. The new builder for that template must set `metadata['python_policy_applied'] = True` (most cleanly via the shared `apply_authored_metadata` helper).

### Step 12: Recipes + materialize-script removal (`recipes/`, `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`, `ready_templates/README.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** `_apply_ltx_policy` in `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py:247-393` is fully covered by `vibecomfy/patches/ltx_lowvram.py` (already singleton-shipped) plus the refactored LTX builders. The other policies (`_apply_flux2_gguf_policy`, `_apply_qwen_image_2512_policy`, `_apply_ace_step_policy`, `_apply_qwen3_tts_policy`, `_apply_wanvideo_wrapper_policy`) become dead — but their **outputs** live in already-serialized `ready_templates/*.py` files and the unrefactored templates still load via `build_api_ready_workflow`, so deletion is safe (Question 4 flags this).
2. **Delete** `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py`. Remove or update the `tests/test_materialize_filtering.py` if it imports from the script.
3. **Create** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/recipes/` with three runnable scripts: `wan_i2v_lowres.py` (`workflow_from_ready('video/wan_i2v')` + `resolution(384, 256, 9).apply(wf)`); `wan_t2v_long.py` (`wan_t2v` + `resolution(832, 480, 81)` + custom seed); `dual_pass_t2i.py` (`z_image` + `subgraph.opaque` upscaling chain placeholder). Each runs via `python recipes/<name>.py` invoking `run_embedded_sync(build())`.
4. **Add** `recipes/README.md` documenting "ready templates change handles, recipes decorate handles".
5. **Update** `ready_templates/README.md` to reference `docs/authoring.md` and the new authoring path.

### Step 13: Phase-1 tests (`tests/test_blocks.py`, `tests/test_patches.py`, `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Extend** `tests/test_blocks.py` (already has 5 tests) with a per-block smoke that asserts compile node count + widget keys for each block in `vibecomfy/blocks/`.
2. **Extend** `tests/test_patches.py` (already exists) with `applies_to` positive + negative + `apply` diff for each builtin patch (`controlnet`, `gguf_unet`, `ltx_lowvram`).
3. **Create** `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py`: build a small workflow via `add_node` + `connect` + `finalize_metadata()`; build the equivalent JSON via `convert_to_vibe_format`; assert `inputs` keys, `outputs` list, and `requirements` are equal.

### Step 14: Documentation extension (`docs/authoring.md`, `AGENTS.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Extend** `docs/authoring.md` (currently 125 lines) with sections covering: Layer 2 verbs (`image.t2i`, `video.t2v`, `video.i2v`, `image.edit`, `edit.qwen`); typed `Artifact`s and the lazy-execution contract; the escape-hatch chain `op() → Artifact → preview_workflow() → VibeWorkflow → compile() → API JSON → run()`; `router.pick(verb, **inputs)`; plugin discovery (project-local + user-global + entry points + `PluginAPI` surface); recipes (with the "decorate handles" rule); `audio.t2a` `NotImplementedError` stub; `--json` contract for the listed commands. Cap combined extension at ~200 lines.
2. **Extend** `AGENTS.md` (currently 35 lines) with a brief Layer 2 contracts section + cross-link to `docs/authoring.md`. Keep it short — agent doc only needs the rules, not examples.

## Phase 4: Acceptance

### Step 15: Acceptance smoke (manual + automated)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap first**: `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x`.
2. **New tests next**: `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x`.
3. **Full suite**: `pytest`.
4. **Spot-check doctor**: `python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py --json` (expect `suggested_patches: []` because LTX patches are already applied) and a hand-stripped variant (expect `ltx_lowvram` suggested).
5. **Plugin smoke**: place a fixture template under `vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/smoke.py` and verify `workflow_from_ready('smoke')`, `workflow_from_template`, `python -m vibecomfy.cli run --ready smoke --dry-run`, and `python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list` all surface it.
6. **Public-import smoke**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any"` exits 0.
7. **Throwaway**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import image; print(list(image.t2i('a tiny smoke test').preview_workflow().compile('api').values())[-1])"` shows a `SaveImage` node, then no file is left behind.
8. **Confirm** `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public surfaces unchanged (diff to confirm no signature/CLI changes).

## Execution Order

1. Phase 1 (typed surface) — prerequisite for ops/router tests and `Artifact` exports.
2. Phase 2 (CLI loader, plugin discovery, `--json`, exports) — `cli_loader` is needed by ops and Phase 3 doctor refactor.
3. Phase 3 (doctor suggestions, template refactor, recipes, tests, docs) — depends on patches + router from earlier phases.
4. Phase 4 (acceptance) — runs after everything lands.

Within Phase 3, refactor templates in the order listed in Step 11 (smallest first, snapshot-diff after each), so a regression localises immediately.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x` — fast, catches block/finalize regressions and snapshot drift.
2. `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x` — new contracts.
3. `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py` — CLI refactor regression coverage.
4. `pytest` — full suite.
5. CLI `--help` smoke for all 17 subcommands.
6. Manual doctor + plugin smoke from Step 15.
7. Confirm `RunPod` smoke at `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` is **not** executed (`@pytest.mark.runpod` opt-in).

  "assumptions": [
    "I will leave `.git/` in place. The brief's instruction \"Repo is NOT a git repo\" is treated as residual text from an earlier worktree (current branch is `codex/composition-ready-templates`). I will not delete the directory or run `git init`.",
    "The `--help` smoke and `--json` rollout target the explicit list in the brief (`sources, workflows, nodes, analyze, search, inspect, convert, validate, doctor, run, runtime, logs, runpod`). The four registered-but-unlisted commands (`fetch, models, session, watchdog`) keep their current output formats unchanged in this plan.",
    "Deleting `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` is acceptable even though it carries non-LTX policy logic (`_apply_qwen_image_2512_policy`, `_apply_ace_step_policy`, `_apply_qwen3_tts_policy`, `_apply_wanvideo_wrapper_policy`, `_apply_flux2_gguf_policy`). The unrefactored ~40 templates load via their already-serialized `API_WORKFLOW`; regenerating them is a follow-up plan's problem. If deletion breaks `tests/test_materialize_filtering.py`, that test is updated.",
    "Refactored builders for the 9 snapshotted templates set `metadata['python_policy_applied'] = True` (via a shared `apply_authored_metadata` helper) so existing test assertions stay green without rewriting tests.",
    "The router's `applicable_patches(workflow) -> list[Patch]` shared probe is a thin re-export of `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable` so router (Layer A) and doctor (Layer C) call the same function. Parametric patches (`resolution(...)`) are not auto-suggested \u2014 only registered builtins (`controlnet`, `gguf_unet`, `ltx_lowvram`) appear in doctor suggestions.",
    "Op signatures `steps`, `fps` map to widget overrides on the existing template's KSampler / CreateVideo nodes (no new patch class needed). `width`, `height`, `length` flow through `vibecomfy.patches.resolution(...)` applied after template load.",
    "The `LTX_smoke` settings in the refactored LTX builder are applied directly inside `build()` (calling `ltx_lowvram.apply` + `resolution(384, 256, 9).apply`) rather than relying on the router. The router is exercised by ops, not by `workflow_from_ready`.",
    "`Artifact` subclasses (`Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`) are tag types \u2014 small subclasses that fix the `kind` literal \u2014 not behavior overrides. No protocol overhead.",
    "The plugin entry-point group `[project.entry-points.\"vibecomfy.plugins\"]` is declared in `pyproject.toml` with a comment-only example payload (no real plugins ship in this PR).",
    "Recipes live at `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/recipes/` (vibecomfy repo root), not at the parent reigh-workspace, per the brief's explicit note.",
    "RunPod smoke (`tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py`, `@pytest.mark.runpod`) stays opt-in and is NOT executed during acceptance \u2014 verified via `pyproject.toml: addopts = \"-m 'not gpu'\"` and the explicit `runpod` marker."
  ],

}

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-010",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "mp-2-implement-p1-hard-gates-20260426-1845"
      ]

  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-006",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "mp-2-implement-p1-hard-gates-20260426-1845"
      ]

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).

# Why this exists

The original plan at `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan/plans/build-the-higher-abstraction-20260425-0953/final.md` was a full 20-task brief that landed about 4 tasks (T1, T2, T5, T10) before its execute step returned `partial` twice and the plan stalled at `state=aborted`. Subsequent work (`design-a-concise-architecture-20260426-1555` → `p1-foundation-typed-handle-20260426-1927`) shipped a typed `Handle` dataclass and `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs).out(...)` authoring API, but did NOT cover the higher-abstraction layer (ops, artifacts, router, recipes, plugin discovery). So the framework exists but the user-facing "ordinary Python pipelines" surface does not.

The original plan doc is the source of truth for design decisions and watch items — read it before planning. This brief deliberately does not restate task details that are still accurate there. Where the original plan's design has been improved by p1-foundation (typed Handle, `wf.node().out()`), prefer the newer API.

# What is already shipped (DO NOT REDO)

Verified by inspection on 2026-04-27:

- `vibecomfy/blocks/` — `__init__.py`, `_utils.py`, `decode.py`, `encoding.py`, `latent.py`, `loaders.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `subgraph.py`, `video.py`. The `Handles` wrapper exists and is typed against the new `Handle` dataclass per p1-foundation.
- `vibecomfy/patches/` — `builtins.py`, `controlnet.py`, `gguf_unet.py`, `ltx_lowvram.py`, `requirements.py`, `resolution.py`, `save_prefix.py`, `seed.py`, `registry.py`, `types.py`. Original T4 patch list (gguf_unet, ltx_lowvram, resolution, seed, save_prefix) plus extras.
- `VibeWorkflow.finalize_metadata()` — `vibecomfy/workflow.py:97`. Helpers in `vibecomfy/metadata.py`.
- `Handle` dataclass + `wf.node(class_type).out(slot)` typed authoring API — `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:16`, `vibecomfy/workflow.py:129`. Use these, not the older "string-only refs" assumption from the original plan.
- `tests/snapshots/` for 8 templates — pre-refactor API + class_type + widget_value snapshots already captured. Ready to drive snapshot-diff tests.
- `docs/authoring.md` (125 lines) — block + patch contracts, finalize_metadata, subgraph.opaque, "changes-handles → new template, decorates-handles → patch" rule. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 (ops/artifacts/router/recipes/plugin discovery) — see C5 below.**
- `AGENTS.md` at repo root. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 — see C5 below.**
- `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` exports `run_embedded`, `run_embedded_sync`, `smoke_runtime`, `smoke_runtime_sync` (T10 done).
- `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` includes the `untyped_raw_ref` lint rule from p1-foundation (separate from T17 patch suggestions, which is still missing).

## Layer A — Typed surface (was original T11–T13)

A1. **`vibecomfy/artifacts.py`** — frozen `Artifact` dataclass (`workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`. Methods: `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow`, `compile() -> dict`, `run(*, runtime='embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.{run_embedded_sync, run_sync}`.

   Note: original T10 listed this as "Depends on T1" and was marked done in `final.md`, but `vibecomfy/artifacts.py` does not exist on disk — verify and ship if missing. (Likely got rolled back when the plan aborted.)

A2. **`vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py` + `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`** — verb-native API returning typed Artifacts, lazy by default. Signatures from original T11:
   - `image.t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `image.edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `video.t2v(prompt, *, model=None, width=None, height=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `video.i2v(image, prompt, *, model=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `audio.t2a(...)` raises `NotImplementedError('no audio template registered')`
   - `edit.qwen(image, instruction, **overrides) -> Image` delegates to `image.edit`

   Op body: pick `(template_id, [patch, ...])` via the router (A3); load via `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)` (B1) or `workflow_from_template`; build VibeWorkflow; apply `set_prompt`/`set_seed` and patches; return typed artifact pointing at the workflow's terminal save node from `outputs`.

A3. **`vibecomfy/router.py` + `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`** — hand-written decision table per original T12. Inputs: `verb`, `inputs` (image presence, requested model, requested resolution/length), `environment` (declared model presence + GPU memory hint, both pluggable; for v1 stub `environment` to `{}` and document that callers can override — exhaustive GPU detection is MP-5 territory).

   **Rules must be exhaustive across all 9 refactored templates** (see C1) — every template must be reachable via at least one rule. The full required rule set covering the 9 templates:
   - `('image', 't2i', model in {'z_image', None}) → 'image/z_image'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf') → 'image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_t2v'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_t2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_i2v'`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_i2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('image', 'edit', model in {'qwen', None}) → 'edit/qwen_image_edit'`
   - `('image', 'edit', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled'`
   - Fall back to `KeyError('no template for verb=...')`. After template selection, call `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)` so router and doctor (C2) share the same probe. Data-driven rules in `router_rules.py` so plugins can extend via `register_route(verb, predicate, template_id, patches=())`.

A4. **Tests**: `tests/test_router.py` (rule table positive/negative + KeyError); `tests/test_ops.py` (`image.t2i('hello')` returns Image without executing; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` has SaveImage and a node carrying the prompt; `preview_workflow().inputs['prompt']` registered — proves finalize_metadata wires through ops). # Critical constraints (from original plan + new context)

- **VibeWorkflow remains the only editable IR.** Blocks must mutate via `add_node`/`connect`; patches must return VibeWorkflow; only `ingest/normalize.py` and `compile()` may edit API dicts directly.
- **Backward compatibility:** existing tests pass unchanged. Existing CLI flag surfaces stay identical. `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public-facing imports/CLI args must not diff.
- **MarkdownNote drop vs snapshot parity:** snapshots in `tests/snapshots/` were captured with MarkdownNote filtered at the source. Refactored templates drop MarkdownNote; snapshot test compares only runnable nodes. If you re-capture without filtering, parity test fails and refactor looks broken.
- **finalize_metadata parity is load-bearing:** `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` must show `inputs/outputs/requirements` equal between block-built workflow + `finalize_metadata()` and `convert_to_vibe_format()` for an equivalent graph. If parity breaks, `set_prompt`/`set_seed`/inspect/op-terminal-artifact-lookup all silently degrade.
- **ltx_lowvram external_python_marker parity:** new patch must produce the same marker key/value as the legacy `_ready_template_policy` mutator before that function is deleted. Verify with `tests/test_ready_templates.py:19` still green.
- **`vibecomfy/cli.py:23 _resolve_workflow_path`** must be either deleted or made to delegate to `load_workflow_any` — duplicate logic in two places will diverge.
- **Plugin discovery cold-start cost:** `_resolve_ready_path` calls `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily; cache empty-plugin result so `workflows list` does not slow on every invocation.
- **Built-in ready-template ids win on duplicate id with plugin templates;** collisions emit a warning, not an exception.
- **Lazy ops contract:** verbs return Artifact objects without executing; `Artifact.run()` triggers execution; `Artifact.preview_workflow()` returns the VibeWorkflow without running. Pipelines compose via file-handoff — single-workflow chaining is out of scope for v1.
- **`audio.t2a` is a `NotImplementedError` stub** until an audio ready template ships. Document in AGENTS.md and `docs/authoring.md`.
- **Each block/patch/op file should stay under ~250 lines** with a single responsibility.
- **`subgraph.opaque` is a passthrough wrapper for UUID-class subgraph nodes;** it cannot be decomposed without unpacking the ComfyUI subgraph definition. Each affected template names the output slot it actually wires (typically slot 0 → 'out').
- **Repo is NOT a git repo.** Do NOT run `git init`. Verify `.git/` does not exist after each phase.

# Out of scope (do NOT do)

- Re-doing anything in the "already shipped" list — verify it still works, but don't reimplement.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` external-mode handling / `ExternalServerRestartRequired` — deferred to MP-5 by p1-foundation.
- Block library expansion beyond what's needed for the 8-template refactor.
- VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration, `wrap_api_dict`, ExternalPythonNode, stage-aware run-dirs / `flow_id` / `stage_index` — MP-5 territory per the design doc.
- Schema-backed validation, `/object_info` snapshot, `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` — MP-6 territory.
- Compile-time serialization gate — MP-3 territory.
- RunPod end-to-end smoke as part of acceptance — `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` exists from p1-foundation but stays opt-in (`@pytest.mark.runpod`); do NOT execute during megaplan run.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Finish Higher-Abstraction Authoring Layer (v2)

## Overview

Adds the user-facing Layer 2 on top of the shipped Layer 1 (`blocks/`, `patches/`, `Handle`, `wf.node().out()`, `finalize_metadata`, snapshots, `docs/authoring.md`). Scope: typed `Artifact`, verb-native ops, hand-written `router`, plugin discovery (with a real op registry), unified CLI loader (layered: `resolve_workflow_path` → path string; `load_workflow_any` → `VibeWorkflow`), `--json` output across the brief's listed commands, doctor patch suggestions, refactor of the 9 snapshotted ready-templates, three example recipes, and the `[entry-points]` group declaration.

This v2 closes 13 significant gate flags. Key changes from v1:
- LTX (and image/edit) refactor now spells out `wf.node()` / `subgraph.opaque` per template with the actual class-type lists from each snapshot — generic block chains alone won't reproduce parity (fixes FLAG-001, correctness-1).
- `resolve_workflow_path` keeps its `str` return; `load_workflow_any` calls it internally as the lower path layer (fixes FLAG-002, correctness-2).
- Plugin `register_op` gets a real op registry consumed by `vibecomfy/ops/` dispatch (fixes FLAG-003, all_locations-1).
- `router.pick` returns `(template_id, explicit_patches, applicable_patches)` so router and doctor share `find_applicable` post-selection (fixes FLAG-004, issue_hints-2).
- Builder/patch contract aligns with current `docs/authoring.md` (any `VibeWorkflow` method, including `disconnect`/`replace_edge`) — older "add_node/connect only" wording removed (fixes issue_hints-1).
- `_finalise_model_assets` migrates to `vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py` before `materialize_ready_templates.py` deletion; `tests/test_materialize_filtering.py` updated to import from the new home (fixes scope-2).
- `analyze info|diff` `--json` documented as alias for the existing `--format json`; new `--json` flag stored separately and resolves to `format='json'` with `--format` winning on conflict (fixes all_locations-2).
- Opaque-subgraph templates (`z_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, the two Flux klein 4b templates) explicitly register the prompt input via a small `register_input(wf, name, node_id, field, value)` helper inside the builder, so `wf.inputs['prompt']` resolves for ops (fixes callers).
- `nodes install/ensure` and `fetch` stay on their current loaders by design — narrowed scope explicitly noted (fixes scope-1).

## Phase 1: Typed Surface (Layer A)

### Step 1: Add `vibecomfy/artifacts.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `Artifact` (frozen dataclass: `workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask` (each subclass fixes the `kind` default; no behavior overrides).
2. **Methods:** `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow` (returns `self.workflow`); `compile() -> dict` (returns `self.workflow.compile('api')`); `run(*, runtime: str = 'embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded_sync` (`'embedded'`) or `vibecomfy.runtime.run_sync` (`'server'`); raise `ValueError` on unknown runtime.
3. **Cap** at ~100 lines.

### Step 2: Op registry (`vibecomfy/ops/registry.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `_OPS: dict[tuple[str, str], Callable] = {}` plus `register_op(verb_kind: str, verb_name: str, fn: Callable)` and `lookup_op(verb_kind, verb_name) -> Callable | None`. This is the consumer for `PluginAPI.register_op` (closes FLAG-003).
2. **Built-in ops** (image.t2i, image.edit, video.t2v, video.i2v, audio.t2a, edit.qwen) self-register at import via `register_op('image', 't2i', t2i)` etc.
3. **Dispatch** in `vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py` exposes `image`, `video`, `audio`, `edit` namespaces. The submodule attribute access first calls the function defined in the submodule; plugins that register over a `(verb_kind, verb_name)` key replace the built-in (warn once on override). Document precedence in C5.

### Step 3: Add the router (`vibecomfy/router.py`, `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `router_rules.py`: a list of `Rule(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches_factory)` plus `register_route(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches=())` for plugin extension. Seed verbatim with the 9 brief rules. LTX rules return `[ltx_lowvram_patch, resolution(384, 256, 9)]` (the `ltx_lowvram` patch is the singleton from `vibecomfy/patches/ltx_lowvram.py:patch`; `resolution` is parametric).
2. **Create** `router.py:pick(verb_kind, verb_name, *, workflow_loader=None, **inputs) -> RouterResult` returning `RouterResult(template_id, explicit_patches, applicable_patches)`. After matching the rule, **mandatorily** load the workflow (using `workflow_loader` if provided, else `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)`), call `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)` (this is the brief's mandated post-selection probe — closes FLAG-004 / issue_hints-2), and include both `explicit_patches` and `applicable_patches` in the result. Doctor (Step 11) consumes the same `find_applicable` directly.
3. **Raise** `KeyError(f'no template for verb={verb_kind!r}/{verb_name!r}')` on miss.
4. **Plugin extension**: `register_route` appends to the rule list at module-import time via `PluginAPI.register_route` (Step 8).

### Step 4: Add `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`vibecomfy/ops/image.py`** — `t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image` and `edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`. Body: call `router.pick('image', 't2i', model=model)`, take `result.template_id` and `result.explicit_patches`, load workflow, `wf.set_prompt(prompt)`, `wf.set_seed(seed)` if not None, apply each explicit patch via `patch.apply(wf)`, apply `resolution(width, height).apply(wf)` only when caller passed non-default `width`/`height`/`length`. Locate terminal node from `wf.outputs` (first `VibeOutput` with `output_type` in `{SaveImage}`); return `Image(workflow=wf, node_id=output.node_id, output_slot=0, kind='image')`.
2. **`vibecomfy/ops/video.py`** — `t2v` and `i2v` mirror; terminal `SaveVideo`. `i2v` takes `image` (path or Artifact) and writes it to the template's `LoadImage.widget_0` (or its equivalent).
3. **`vibecomfy/ops/audio.py`** — `t2a(prompt, **kw)` raises `NotImplementedError("no audio template registered")` (per brief).
4. **`vibecomfy/ops/edit.py`** — `qwen(image, instruction, **overrides)` delegates to `image.edit(model='qwen', ...)`.
5. **Self-register** each op via `vibecomfy.ops.registry.register_op('image', 't2i', t2i)` at module bottom.

### Step 5: Router + ops tests (`tests/test_router.py`, `tests/test_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`tests/test_router.py`** — one positive test per of the 9 brief rules asserting expected `template_id` and `explicit_patches` names; one negative `KeyError`; one assertion that `result.applicable_patches` is a `list[Patch]` populated from `find_applicable` (so the shared-probe contract is exercised).
2. **`tests/test_ops.py`** — `image.t2i('hello')` returns an `Image` without executing; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` contains a `SaveImage` node and `wf.inputs['prompt']` is registered. Skip end-to-end runs.

## Phase 2: CLI Unification + Plugin Discovery (Layer B)

### Step 6: Layered loader (`vibecomfy/cli_loader.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Keep** `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py:resolve_workflow_path(value: str) -> str` unchanged in signature and return type — it remains the path-resolution layer (JSON-index lookup + ready-template id → file path + scratchpad/json path passthrough). Existing callers (`inspect.py`, `convert.py`, `fetch.py`, `doctor._json_path_for_reference`, `tests/test_cli.py`) keep working byte-for-byte (closes FLAG-002 / correctness-2).
2. **Extend** `resolve_workflow_path` to additionally accept ready-template ids by checking `vibecomfy.registry.ready.ready_template_ids()` and returning the resolved Python file path (re-using `_resolve_ready_path`). This unifies id → path resolution without breaking the string contract.
3. **Create** `vibecomfy/cli_loader.py:load_workflow_any(path_or_id: str) -> VibeWorkflow` as the *higher* layer:
   - If `value` matches a ready-template id → `workflow_from_ready(value)`.
   - Else `path = resolve_workflow_path(value)` (raises `FileNotFoundError`); dispatch on suffix: `.py` → `load_scratchpad(path)`; `.json` → `convert_to_vibe_format(normalize_to_api(load_template(path), schema_provider=get_schema_provider('auto')), source_path=path, schema_provider=get_schema_provider('auto'))`.
4. **`tests/test_cli_loader.py`** covers ready-id, slash-id, scratchpad path, JSON path, missing-id `FileNotFoundError`/`KeyError`.

### Step 7: Refactor commands to use `load_workflow_any` (`vibecomfy/commands/{doctor,inspect,run,validate,convert}.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** the workflow-construction arm in `doctor.py`, `inspect.py`, `run.py`, `validate.py`, `convert.py` with `load_workflow_any(args.path)`. Helpers that need a path string (`doctor._json_path_for_reference`, `convert.render_scratchpad`) keep calling `resolve_workflow_path` directly — this is the layered split, not duplicated logic.
2. **Out of scope (documented):** `nodes install/ensure` (`vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py`) and `fetch.py` keep their existing `load_workflow_reference` / `resolve_workflow_path` calls. The brief lists the five commands that must move to the new loader; widening to `nodes`/`fetch` is a follow-up. This is the explicit response to scope-1.
3. **Run** `pytest tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py` after this step.

### Step 8: Plugin discovery (`vibecomfy/extras.py`, `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/extras.py`:
   - `PluginAPI` — small dataclass exposing `register_block(name, fn)`, `register_patch(patch)`, `register_op(verb_kind, verb_name, fn)`, `register_route(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches=())`, `register_ready_root(path)`. Each method routes to the underlying registry (`vibecomfy.blocks._BLOCK_REGISTRY`, `vibecomfy.patches.registry.register`, `vibecomfy.ops.registry.register_op`, `vibecomfy.router_rules.register_route`, `_EXTRA_READY_ROOTS` list).
   - `_LOADED: bool = False`, `_API: PluginAPI | None = None`.
   - `load_plugins() -> PluginAPI` walks the three sources: project-local `./vibecomfy_extras/{blocks,patches,ops,recipes,ready_templates}/*.py` (importlib spec_from_file_location); user-global `Path.home() / '.vibecomfy' / {...}/*.py`; `importlib.metadata.entry_points(group='vibecomfy.plugins')` calling each `ep.load()(api)`.
   - `ensure_plugins_loaded() -> PluginAPI` calls `load_plugins()` once and caches even an empty result.
2. **Refactor** `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`:
   - Replace module-level `READY_ROOT` with `_ready_roots() -> list[Path]` returning built-in repo `ready_templates/`, `Path.cwd() / 'vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates'`, `Path.home() / '.vibecomfy/ready_templates'`, plus `register_ready_root` paths.
   - `ready_template_ids()` walks all roots, builds an id→path dict, built-in winning on duplicate (`warnings.warn` once per collision, not exception).
   - `_resolve_ready_path` walks all roots in priority order.
   - Both call `vibecomfy.extras.ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily; the empty-cache guarantees a single import-time hit.
3. **Expose** `ensure_plugins_loaded` from `vibecomfy/__init__.py` (Step 10).
4. **`tests/test_plugin_discovery.py`**: three cases (project-local fixture under `tmp_path/vibecomfy_extras/...`; user-global fixture via `monkeypatch.setattr(Path, 'home', ...)`; entry-point fixture via `importlib.metadata.entry_points` mocking). Each registers one of: block, patch, op, route, ready-root; assertions cover discovery, dispatch (`vibecomfy.ops.image.<plugin_verb>` works), and id-collision warning.

### Step 9: `--json` output (`vibecomfy/commands/_output.py` + listed command modules)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/commands/_output.py:emit(payload, *, json: bool, text_renderer)`. JSON path uses `json.dumps(..., default=str, indent=2)`; text path calls `text_renderer(payload)` (preserves existing per-command formatting).
2. **Add `--json`** to: `workflows list`, `nodes list`, `inspect`, `doctor`, `sources sync` (summary), `analyze info`, `analyze diff`. For `analyze info|diff`, **`--json` is an alias for `--format json`**: `parser.add_argument('--json', action='store_true', dest='_json_alias')`; in `_cmd_*` set `args.format = 'json' if args._json_alias and args.format == 'text' else args.format`. If both `--json` and `--format` are supplied with conflicting values, **`--format` wins** (last-explicit-flag rule); document in `--json` help text. This closes all_locations-2.
3. **`inspect --json` payload** includes `"applicable_patches": [{"name": ..., "rationale": ...}]` from `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)`.
4. **`doctor --json` payload** includes `"suggested_patches": [...]` from the same `find_applicable` call (Step 11).

### Step 10: Public exports + entry-point group (`vibecomfy/__init__.py`, `pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `vibecomfy/__init__.py` to additionally export: `Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any`. Keep existing exports.
2. **Add** to `pyproject.toml`:
   ```toml
   [project.entry-points."vibecomfy.plugins"]
   # External packages register plugins here:
   #   my_plugin = "my_pkg:register"
   ```
3. **Smoke** `python -m vibecomfy.cli {sources,workflows,nodes,analyze,search,inspect,convert,validate,doctor,run,runtime,logs,runpod} --help` exits 0 (the brief's 14; `fetch/models/session/watchdog` stay unchanged per Step 7's documented narrowing).

**Scope:** Small

**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `VibeWorkflow.register_input(name: str, node_id: str, field: str, value: Any) -> VibeWorkflow` (small wrapper that writes `self.inputs[name] = VibeInput(name, node_id, field, value)` and returns self). Builders that wrap a UUID subgraph (where `_register_common_inputs` won't auto-detect a prompt) call this directly to make `wf.set_prompt(...)` and `wf.inputs['prompt']` work. Closes the `callers` flag.
2. **Add** `apply_authored_metadata(workflow, ready_metadata, *, requirements, source_path) -> VibeWorkflow` to `vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py` — extracts the metadata-merge from `apply_ready_template_policy` (sets `metadata.update(...)`, `metadata['ready_template_path']`, `metadata['python_policy_applied'] = True`, calls `_merge_requirements`). Refactored builders call this after composing nodes so the existing `python_policy_applied` assertion stays green.

### Step 14: Refactor 9 ready_templates onto blocks (`ready_templates/{video,image,edit}/<id>.py`)
**Scope:** Large

**Per-template construction strategy** — each template's class types come from its snapshot. Use `subgraph.opaque` for UUID class types and `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs).out(slot)` for everything else (these are valid per the *current* `docs/authoring.md`, which explicitly permits any `VibeWorkflow` method including `disconnect`/`replace_edge`; closes issue_hints-1). Each builder ends with `wf.finalize_metadata()` then `apply_authored_metadata(wf, READY_METADATA, requirements=READY_REQUIREMENTS, source_path=__file__)`.

**Order: smallest first; run snapshot test after each.**

1. **`image/z_image.py`** (snapshot 27 lines, 4 class types). Compose: `LoadNothing` not needed; `subgraph.opaque(class_type='9b9009e4-...', widgets_by_name={...full prompt + size widgets...}, outputs=('image',))` → `wf.node('SaveImage', images=opaque.image, widget_0='z-image').out(0)`. **Critical:** call `wf.register_input('prompt', opaque_node_id, 'widget_0', PROMPT_TEXT)` so ops' `set_prompt`/`wf.inputs['prompt']` works (UUID class isn't in `PROMPT_NODE_CLASSES`). Drop MarkdownNote.
2. **`image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py`** (snapshot 58 lines). Use `wf.node()` for the explicit class types (CLIPTextEncode, CheckpointLoaderSimple, samplers, etc — read the snapshot to get the full list before writing the builder).
3. **`image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py`** (snapshot 16 lines — small).
4. **`edit/qwen_image_edit.py`** (snapshot 40 lines, 4 class types). UUID `74a8e1e2-...` via `subgraph.opaque`, `LoadImage`, `ImageScaleToTotalPixels`, `SaveImage` via `wf.node()`. Register prompt input on the subgraph (its `widget_0` carries the instruction text).
5. **`edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled.py`** (snapshot 58 lines).
6. **`video/wan_t2v.py`** (snapshot 125 lines, 10 class types — all explicit, no UUIDs). Standard `wf.node()` chain: CLIPLoader, VAELoader, UNETLoader, CLIPTextEncode (×2), EmptyHunyuanLatentVideo, ModelSamplingSD3, KSampler, VAEDecode, CreateVideo, SaveVideo. CLIPTextEncode auto-registers prompt via `PROMPT_NODE_CLASSES`.
7. **`video/wan_i2v.py`** (snapshot 172 lines).
8. **`video/ltx2_3_t2v.py`** (snapshot 559 lines, 33 class types: **CFGGuider, CLIPTextEncode, ClownSampler_Beta, CreateVideo (×2), EmptyLTXVLatentVideo, GemmaAPITextEncode, GuiderParameters, KSamplerSelect, LTXAVTextEncoderLoader, LTXFloatToInt, LTXVAudioVAEDecode, LTXVConcatAVLatent, LTXVConditioning, LTXVEmptyLatentAudio, LTXVImgToVideoConditionOnly, LTXVPreprocess, LTXVScheduler, LTXVSeparateAVLatent, LTXVTiledVAEDecode, LoadImage, LoraLoaderModelOnly, LowVRAMAudioVAELoader, LowVRAMCheckpointLoader, ManualSigmas, MultimodalGuider, PrimitiveBoolean/Float/Int/String, RandomNoise, ResizeImageMaskNode, SamplerCustomAdvanced, SaveVideo (×2)**). **No UUID subgraphs** — every class is named, so `wf.node()` covers all of them. The builder is mechanical: read each node from the snapshot, instantiate via `wf.node(class_type, **widget_or_input_kwargs).out(0)`, wire edges via `Handle`s. Two terminal SaveVideo branches → use `wf.outputs[0]` for the primary video output; document in builder comments. Apply `ltx_lowvram.apply(wf)` and `resolution(384, 256, 9).apply(wf)` inline so `compile()` matches the snapshot.

   **Block-library expansion is out of scope:** do not add new generic blocks for these LTX classes; use `wf.node()` directly per the brief's "Block library expansion beyond what's needed for the 8-template refactor" out-of-scope rule. Closes FLAG-001 / correctness-1.
9. **`video/ltx2_3_i2v.py`** (snapshot 559 lines — same class-type set as t2v). Use the same `wf.node()` mechanical approach.

**Snapshot diff test** (`tests/test_ready_templates.py`): add `test_refactored_template_matches_snapshot` parametrized over the 9 ids. For each id: load `tests/snapshots/<id>.api.json`, build via `workflow_from_ready`, compare `compile('api')` with node-id reordering allowed and MarkdownNote filtered out. Comparison key: `frozenset((class_type, frozenset(widget_value_items)) for node in api.values())` plus a topology hash (set of `(from_class, from_slot, to_class, to_input)` after node-id reordering). Do **not** re-capture snapshots.

**Do not break** `test_all_ready_templates_load_and_validate` (loads ALL 49 templates) — the unrefactored ~40 still go through `build_api_ready_workflow`.

### Step 18: Acceptance smoke (manual + automated)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap first**: `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_ready_templates.py tests/test_materialize_filtering.py -x`.
2. **New tests**: `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x`.
3. **CLI regression**: `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py`.
4. **Full suite**: `pytest`.
5. **Spot-check doctor**: `python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py --json` (expect `suggested_patches: []`); hand-stripped variant (expect `ltx_lowvram` suggested).
6. **Plugin smoke**: drop a fixture template under `vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/smoke.py`; verify `workflow_from_ready('smoke')`, `python -m vibecomfy.cli run --ready smoke --dry-run`, and `python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list` surface it.
7. **Public-import smoke**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any"` exits 0.
8. **Throwaway**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import image; print(list(image.t2i('a tiny smoke test').preview_workflow().compile('api').values())[-1])"` shows a `SaveImage` node.
9. **Confirm** `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public surfaces unchanged via `git diff` (or equivalent file diff against the current branch baseline).

## Execution Order

1. Phase 1 (artifacts → op registry → router → ops → tests). Op registry must precede ops modules so they can self-register at import.
2. Phase 2 in order: Step 6 (loader layering) → Step 7 (command refactor — depends on `load_workflow_any`) → Step 8 (plugin discovery — uses op registry from Step 2 and ready-roots refactor) → Step 9 (`--json`) → Step 10 (exports + entry-point group).
3. Phase 3: Step 11 (doctor) → Step 12 (move `_finalise_model_assets`) → Step 13 (`register_input` + `apply_authored_metadata`) → Step 14 (template refactor — runs snapshot test after each template) → Step 15 (delete script + recipes) → Step 16 (tests) → Step 17 (docs).
4. Phase 4 (acceptance) last.

Within Step 14, refactor templates in the order listed (smallest first) so a regression localises immediately.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_materialize_filtering.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x` — fast, catches block/finalize/snapshot regressions and the moved `_finalise_model_assets` import.
2. `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x` — new contracts.
3. `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py` — CLI refactor regression coverage.
4. `pytest` — full suite.
5. CLI `--help` smoke for the brief's 14 subcommands.
6. Manual doctor + plugin smoke from Step 18.
7. Confirm `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` and `@pytest.mark.runpod` tests are NOT executed.

I verified the prior fixes that are genuinely addressed (`FLAG-001` through `FLAG-004` plus related prior findings), and added new flags:

- `FLAG-005`: `SaveVideo` is not registered in `OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES`, so video ops cannot find terminal outputs via `wf.outputs`.
- `FLAG-006`: prompt/input registration is still wrong or impossible for several opaque/edit templates based on the actual snapshots.
- `FLAG-007`: router `applicable_patches` expectations conflict with already-patched LTX ready templates.
- `FLAG-008`: acceptance smoke uses `run --dry-run`, but the run command has no such flag.
- `FLAG-009`: multi-root ready templates need `_template_id_for_path` updated, not just `_resolve_ready_path` / `ready_template_ids`.

The JSON validates with `jq`.

# Why this exists

The original plan at `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan/plans/build-the-higher-abstraction-20260425-0953/final.md` was a full 20-task brief that landed about 4 tasks (T1, T2, T5, T10) before its execute step returned `partial` twice and the plan stalled at `state=aborted`. Subsequent work (`design-a-concise-architecture-20260426-1555` → `p1-foundation-typed-handle-20260426-1927`) shipped a typed `Handle` dataclass and `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs).out(...)` authoring API, but did NOT cover the higher-abstraction layer (ops, artifacts, router, recipes, plugin discovery). So the framework exists but the user-facing "ordinary Python pipelines" surface does not.

The original plan doc is the source of truth for design decisions and watch items — read it before planning. This brief deliberately does not restate task details that are still accurate there. Where the original plan's design has been improved by p1-foundation (typed Handle, `wf.node().out()`), prefer the newer API.

# What is already shipped (DO NOT REDO)

Verified by inspection on 2026-04-27:

- `vibecomfy/blocks/` — `__init__.py`, `_utils.py`, `decode.py`, `encoding.py`, `latent.py`, `loaders.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `subgraph.py`, `video.py`. The `Handles` wrapper exists and is typed against the new `Handle` dataclass per p1-foundation.
- `vibecomfy/patches/` — `builtins.py`, `controlnet.py`, `gguf_unet.py`, `ltx_lowvram.py`, `requirements.py`, `resolution.py`, `save_prefix.py`, `seed.py`, `registry.py`, `types.py`. Original T4 patch list (gguf_unet, ltx_lowvram, resolution, seed, save_prefix) plus extras.
- `VibeWorkflow.finalize_metadata()` — `vibecomfy/workflow.py:97`. Helpers in `vibecomfy/metadata.py`.
- `Handle` dataclass + `wf.node(class_type).out(slot)` typed authoring API — `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:16`, `vibecomfy/workflow.py:129`. Use these, not the older "string-only refs" assumption from the original plan.
- `tests/snapshots/` for 8 templates — pre-refactor API + class_type + widget_value snapshots already captured. Ready to drive snapshot-diff tests.
- `docs/authoring.md` (125 lines) — block + patch contracts, finalize_metadata, subgraph.opaque, "changes-handles → new template, decorates-handles → patch" rule. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 (ops/artifacts/router/recipes/plugin discovery) — see C5 below.**
- `AGENTS.md` at repo root. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 — see C5 below.**
- `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` exports `run_embedded`, `run_embedded_sync`, `smoke_runtime`, `smoke_runtime_sync` (T10 done).
- `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` includes the `untyped_raw_ref` lint rule from p1-foundation (separate from T17 patch suggestions, which is still missing).

# What is still missing (THE SCOPE OF THIS PLAN)

Listed in dependency order. The original `final.md` task numbers are referenced for cross-checking; do not re-derive scope from scratch.

## Layer A — Typed surface (was original T11–T13)

A1. **`vibecomfy/artifacts.py`** — frozen `Artifact` dataclass (`workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`. Methods: `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow`, `compile() -> dict`, `run(*, runtime='embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.{run_embedded_sync, run_sync}`.

   Note: original T10 listed this as "Depends on T1" and was marked done in `final.md`, but `vibecomfy/artifacts.py` does not exist on disk — verify and ship if missing. (Likely got rolled back when the plan aborted.)

A2. **`vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py` + `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`** — verb-native API returning typed Artifacts, lazy by default. Signatures from original T11:
   - `image.t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `image.edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `video.t2v(prompt, *, model=None, width=None, height=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `video.i2v(image, prompt, *, model=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `audio.t2a(...)` raises `NotImplementedError('no audio template registered')`
   - `edit.qwen(image, instruction, **overrides) -> Image` delegates to `image.edit`

   Op body: pick `(template_id, [patch, ...])` via the router (A3); load via `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)` (B1) or `workflow_from_template`; build VibeWorkflow; apply `set_prompt`/`set_seed` and patches; return typed artifact pointing at the workflow's terminal save node from `outputs`.

A3. **`vibecomfy/router.py` + `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`** — hand-written decision table per original T12. Inputs: `verb`, `inputs` (image presence, requested model, requested resolution/length), `environment` (declared model presence + GPU memory hint, both pluggable; for v1 stub `environment` to `{}` and document that callers can override — exhaustive GPU detection is MP-5 territory).

   **Rules must be exhaustive across all 9 refactored templates** (see C1) — every template must be reachable via at least one rule. The full required rule set covering the 9 templates:
   - `('image', 't2i', model in {'z_image', None}) → 'image/z_image'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf') → 'image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_t2v'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_t2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_i2v'`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_i2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('image', 'edit', model in {'qwen', None}) → 'edit/qwen_image_edit'`
   - `('image', 'edit', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled'`
   - Fall back to `KeyError('no template for verb=...')`. After template selection, call `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)` so router and doctor (C2) share the same probe. Data-driven rules in `router_rules.py` so plugins can extend via `register_route(verb, predicate, template_id, patches=())`.

A4. **Tests**: `tests/test_router.py` (rule table positive/negative + KeyError); `tests/test_ops.py` (`image.t2i('hello')` returns Image without executing; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` has SaveImage and a node carrying the prompt; `preview_workflow().inputs['prompt']` registered — proves finalize_metadata wires through ops). Skip end-to-end runs in CI.

# Critical constraints (from original plan + new context)

- **VibeWorkflow remains the only editable IR.** Blocks must mutate via `add_node`/`connect`; patches must return VibeWorkflow; only `ingest/normalize.py` and `compile()` may edit API dicts directly.
- **Backward compatibility:** existing tests pass unchanged. Existing CLI flag surfaces stay identical. `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public-facing imports/CLI args must not diff.
- **MarkdownNote drop vs snapshot parity:** snapshots in `tests/snapshots/` were captured with MarkdownNote filtered at the source. Refactored templates drop MarkdownNote; snapshot test compares only runnable nodes. If you re-capture without filtering, parity test fails and refactor looks broken.
- **finalize_metadata parity is load-bearing:** `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` must show `inputs/outputs/requirements` equal between block-built workflow + `finalize_metadata()` and `convert_to_vibe_format()` for an equivalent graph. If parity breaks, `set_prompt`/`set_seed`/inspect/op-terminal-artifact-lookup all silently degrade.
- **ltx_lowvram external_python_marker parity:** new patch must produce the same marker key/value as the legacy `_ready_template_policy` mutator before that function is deleted. Verify with `tests/test_ready_templates.py:19` still green.
- **`vibecomfy/cli.py:23 _resolve_workflow_path`** must be either deleted or made to delegate to `load_workflow_any` — duplicate logic in two places will diverge.
- **Plugin discovery cold-start cost:** `_resolve_ready_path` calls `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily; cache empty-plugin result so `workflows list` does not slow on every invocation.
- **Built-in ready-template ids win on duplicate id with plugin templates;** collisions emit a warning, not an exception.
- **Lazy ops contract:** verbs return Artifact objects without executing; `Artifact.run()` triggers execution; `Artifact.preview_workflow()` returns the VibeWorkflow without running. Pipelines compose via file-handoff — single-workflow chaining is out of scope for v1.
- **`audio.t2a` is a `NotImplementedError` stub** until an audio ready template ships. Document in AGENTS.md and `docs/authoring.md`.
- **Each block/patch/op file should stay under ~250 lines** with a single responsibility.
- **`subgraph.opaque` is a passthrough wrapper for UUID-class subgraph nodes;** it cannot be decomposed without unpacking the ComfyUI subgraph definition. Each affected template names the output slot it actually wires (typically slot 0 → 'out').
- **Repo is NOT a git repo.** Do NOT run `git init`. Verify `.git/` does not exist after each phase.

# Out of scope (do NOT do)

- Re-doing anything in the "already shipped" list — verify it still works, but don't reimplement.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` external-mode handling / `ExternalServerRestartRequired` — deferred to MP-5 by p1-foundation.
- Block library expansion beyond what's needed for the 8-template refactor.
- VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration, `wrap_api_dict`, ExternalPythonNode, stage-aware run-dirs / `flow_id` / `stage_index` — MP-5 territory per the design doc.
- Schema-backed validation, `/object_info` snapshot, `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` — MP-6 territory.
- Compile-time serialization gate — MP-3 territory.
- RunPod end-to-end smoke as part of acceptance — `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` exists from p1-foundation but stays opt-in (`@pytest.mark.runpod`); do NOT execute during megaplan run.
