# Acceptance

- `from vibecomfy import Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any` succeeds.
- `image.t2i('hello').preview_workflow().compile('api')` returns a dict with a SaveImage node and the prompt registered as an input.
- `router.pick('video', 'i2v', model='ltx')` returns the LTX template + lowvram + resolution patches.
- `vibecomfy doctor` (text + `--json`) lists applicable patches; zero on a policy-applied LTX template, `ltx_lowvram` on a stripped one.
- All 9 snapshotted ready_templates compile to a snapshot-equivalent graph (modulo node-id reordering + MarkdownNote stripping). The other ~40 unrefactored templates still load and run unchanged.
- The router has at least one rule reaching every one of the 9 refactored templates; `tests/test_router.py` covers each one.
- `vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/` fixture template is resolvable through `workflow_from_ready`, `workflow_from_template`, `run --ready`, `workflows list`.
- `python -m vibecomfy.cli {sources,workflows,nodes,analyze,search,inspect,convert,validate,doctor,run,runtime,logs,runpod} --help` exits 0 for every subcommand.
- `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` deleted; `recipes/` exists at vibecomfy repo root with three runnable scripts.
- `docs/authoring.md` and `AGENTS.md` describe Layer 2 (ops, artifacts, router, recipes, plugin discovery, escape-hatch chain, `audio.t2a` stub, `--json` contract).
- `vibecomfy/pyproject.toml` declares the `[project.entry-points."vibecomfy.plugins"]` group.
- Full pytest suite passes. RunPod smoke remains opt-in and unexecuted.
- `.git/` still absent.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Finish Higher-Abstraction Authoring Layer (v3)

## Overview

Layer 2 on top of the shipped Layer 1. Scope unchanged: typed `Artifact`, verb-native ops, hand-written `router`, plugin discovery (with op registry), layered loader (`resolve_workflow_path` returns `str`; `load_workflow_any` returns `VibeWorkflow`), `--json` output across the brief's 14 commands, doctor patch suggestions, refactor of the 9 snapshotted ready-templates, three example recipes, and the `[entry-points]` group declaration.

v3 closes 11 v2-surfaced flags. None suggest a wrong root cause — they are concrete code-level corrections within the same scope. Five additions to v2:
- **FLAG-005 / correctness-1**: extend `vibecomfy/metadata.py:OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES` to include `SaveVideo`, `SaveAudio`, `SaveAudioMP3`, `CreateVideo` (terminal video synthesis when no SaveVideo follows). Without this, `wf.outputs` is empty for video templates and `video.t2v/i2v` ops can't build `Video` artifacts.
- **FLAG-006 / correctness-2 / callers**: per-template prompt-field strategy — `register_input` targets the **prompt-bearing node**, which differs by template. Spelled out per template in Step 14. For templates with no prompt widget (`qwen_image_edit`, `flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled`), `image.edit(instruction=...)` is documented as a no-op (image-only edit) — the templates take an image and emit an edited image; no instruction text is editable in the snapshotted graph. Callers wanting instruction control use a different model.
- **FLAG-007 / correctness-3**: `RouterResult.applicable_patches` semantics clarified as the **gap** (still-applicable patches per `find_applicable`). For as-shipped templates that already applied policy patches inline, this is `[]` — which is consistent with doctor's "0 suggestions on as-shipped LTX template". Tests assert `applicable_patches is a list`, not `populated`.
- **FLAG-008 / issue_hints**: replace acceptance smoke `vibecomfy run --ready smoke --dry-run` (flag does not exist) with `vibecomfy inspect smoke` (existing command).
- **FLAG-009 / scope**: `_template_id_for_path` in `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py` walks `_ready_roots()` and returns the id relative to the matching root, so plugin/user/project ready-template paths populate `metadata['ready_template']` correctly.

All v2 settled decisions (SD-001 through SD-014) carry forward unchanged.

## Phase 1: Typed Surface (Layer A)

### Step 1: Add `vibecomfy/artifacts.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `Artifact` (frozen dataclass: `workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`.
2. **Methods:** `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow`; `compile() -> dict` (`self.workflow.compile('api')`); `run(*, runtime: str = 'embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded_sync`/`run_sync`; raise `ValueError` on unknown runtime.
3. **Cap** ~100 lines.

### Step 2: Op registry (`vibecomfy/ops/registry.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `_OPS: dict[tuple[str, str], Callable] = {}`, `register_op(verb_kind, verb_name, fn)`, `lookup_op(verb_kind, verb_name) -> Callable | None`.
2. **Built-ins** in Step 4 self-register at import time.
3. **Dispatch** in `vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py`: `image`, `video`, `audio`, `edit` namespaces resolve `(verb_kind, verb_name)` against the registry; built-ins call the submodule function, plugin overrides win with a one-time `warnings.warn`.

### Step 3: Extend output registration (`vibecomfy/metadata.py`) — required for video ops
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `SaveVideo`, `SaveAudio`, `SaveAudioMP3` to `OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES` at `vibecomfy/metadata.py:13`. (Note: `CreateVideo` is intentionally NOT added — it produces a video object that flows into a downstream `SaveVideo`; `CreateVideo` without a `SaveVideo` is an in-progress chain and shouldn't be the artifact target.)
2. **Verify** post-change: `workflow_from_ready('video/wan_t2v').outputs` contains a `VibeOutput(output_type='SaveVideo')`. This is a **prerequisite** for video ops to find their terminal node. Closes FLAG-005 / correctness-1.
3. **Test** in Step 16 (`tests/test_finalize_metadata.py`) asserts `SaveVideo` registers as an output for a small `wf.add_node('SaveVideo', ...)` workflow.

### Step 4: Add the router (`vibecomfy/router.py`, `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`router_rules.py`**: list of `Rule(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches_factory)` plus `register_route(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches=())`. Seed with the 9 brief rules; LTX rules return `[ltx_lowvram_patch, resolution(384, 256, 9)]`.
2. **`router.py:pick(verb_kind, verb_name, *, workflow_loader=None, **inputs) -> RouterResult`** returning `RouterResult(template_id: str, explicit_patches: list[Patch], applicable_patches: list[Patch])`. After matching the rule, mandatorily load the workflow (default `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)`), then call `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)`.
3. **Semantics clarification** (closes FLAG-007 / correctness-3): `applicable_patches` is the **gap** — patches whose `applies_to(workflow)` returns `True`, i.e. patches the workflow could still benefit from. For as-shipped templates that already applied their policy patches inline (LTX templates with `ltx_lowvram` baked in), `applicable_patches` is **expected to be `[]`**. This matches the doctor "0 suggestions on as-shipped LTX template" criterion. Document this in `RouterResult.__doc__`.
4. **Raise** `KeyError` on miss.

### Step 5: Add `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`image.py`** — `t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image`; `edit(image, instruction=None, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`. Body: `result = router.pick(...)`; load workflow; `wf.set_prompt(prompt)` (only if `'prompt' in wf.inputs` — see Step 14 prompt-field strategy); `wf.set_seed(seed)` if not None; apply each `result.explicit_patches`; apply `resolution(width, height).apply(wf)` only when caller passed non-default values; locate terminal `SaveImage` from `wf.outputs[0]`; return `Image(workflow=wf, node_id=output.node_id, output_slot=0, kind='image')`.
2. **`video.py`** — `t2v` and `i2v`; locate terminal `SaveVideo` from `wf.outputs` (relies on Step 3 `OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES` extension). For LTX templates with two `SaveVideo` branches, pick the first `VibeOutput` (sorted by node-id from `finalize_metadata`); document in builder.
3. **`audio.py`** — `t2a(prompt, **kw)` raises `NotImplementedError("no audio template registered")`.
4. **`edit.py`** — `qwen(image, instruction=None, **overrides)` delegates to `image.edit(model='qwen', ...)`. Note: for `model='qwen'` the underlying template is image-only — `instruction` is silently ignored (warn once).
5. **Self-register** each op via `vibecomfy.ops.registry.register_op(...)` at module bottom.

### Step 6: Router + ops tests (`tests/test_router.py`, `tests/test_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`tests/test_router.py`** — one positive test per of the 9 rules asserting `result.template_id` and `result.explicit_patches` names; one negative `KeyError`; one assertion that `result.applicable_patches` is a `list[Patch]` (typed). For LTX rule, additionally assert `applicable_patches == []` (because `ltx_lowvram` is already inline). Closes FLAG-007.
2. **`tests/test_ops.py`** — `image.t2i('hello')` returns `Image` lazily; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` contains `SaveImage`; `wf.inputs['prompt']` is registered (proves Step 14 prompt-field wiring + Step 3 output registration).

Document in C5 docs. |

5. **Spot-check doctor**: `python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py --json` (expect `suggested_patches: []`); hand-stripped variant (expect `ltx_lowvram` suggested).
6. **Plugin smoke**: drop `vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/smoke.py`; verify `python -c "from vibecomfy.registry.ready import workflow_from_ready; wf = workflow_from_ready('smoke'); print(wf.metadata['ready_template'])"` prints `smoke`; `python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list | grep smoke`; `python -m vibecomfy.cli inspect smoke` exits 0 (replaces the broken `run --ready smoke --dry-run` from v2 — `inspect` is an existing command that loads + summarizes a workflow without invoking the runtime; closes FLAG-008 / issue_hints).
7. **Public-import smoke**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any"` exits 0.
8. **Throwaway**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import image; print(list(image.t2i('a tiny smoke test').preview_workflow().compile('api').values())[-1])"` shows a `SaveImage` node.
9. **Confirm** `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public surfaces unchanged via file diff against the current branch baseline.

## Execution Order

1. Phase 1: Step 1 (artifacts) → Step 2 (op registry) → **Step 3 (output_node_names extension — load-bearing prerequisite for video ops)** → Step 4 (router) → Step 5 (ops) → Step 6 (tests).
2. Phase 2: Step 7 (loader) → Step 8 (command refactor) → Step 9 (plugin discovery + multi-root id resolution) → Step 10 (`--json`) → Step 11 (exports + entry-point).
3. Phase 3: Step 12 (doctor) → Step 13 (move `_finalise_model_assets`) → Step 14 (helpers + 9 templates, smallest first) → Step 15 (delete script + recipes) → Step 16 (tests) → Step 17 (docs).
4. Phase 4 last.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_materialize_filtering.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x` — fast; catches block/finalize/snapshot regressions, the moved `_finalise_model_assets` import, and the `SaveVideo` output registration.
2. `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x` — new contracts (router result shape, ops + video output discovery, plugin id resolution).
3. `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py` — CLI refactor regression.
4. `pytest` — full suite.
5. CLI `--help` smoke for the brief's 14 subcommands.
6. Manual doctor + plugin smoke from Step 18 (using `inspect smoke`, not `run --dry-run`).
7. Confirm `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` and `@pytest.mark.runpod` tests are NOT executed.

Therefore router.pick('video', 'i2v', model='ltx') loading the ready template will likely return applicable_patches == [], contradicting the v2 success criterion that it is populated from find_applicable while also preserving the doctor requirement of zero suggestions on as-shipped LTX templates.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: Ready-template plugin roots: checked vibecomfy/registry/ready.py and found workflow_from_ready still calls _template_id_for_path(path), which currently computes the id relative to the single built-in READY_ROOT. V2 refactors ready_template_ids and _resolve_ready_path for multiple roots but does not explicitly update _template_id_for_path to identify the matching root, so workflow_from_ready on a project-local/user/plugin ready template path outside the built-in root would fail when setting workflow.metadata['ready_template'].",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  }
]

- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

# Why this exists

The original plan at `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan/plans/build-the-higher-abstraction-20260425-0953/final.md` was a full 20-task brief that landed about 4 tasks (T1, T2, T5, T10) before its execute step returned `partial` twice and the plan stalled at `state=aborted`. Subsequent work (`design-a-concise-architecture-20260426-1555` → `p1-foundation-typed-handle-20260426-1927`) shipped a typed `Handle` dataclass and `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs).out(...)` authoring API, but did NOT cover the higher-abstraction layer (ops, artifacts, router, recipes, plugin discovery). So the framework exists but the user-facing "ordinary Python pipelines" surface does not.

The original plan doc is the source of truth for design decisions and watch items — read it before planning. This brief deliberately does not restate task details that are still accurate there. Where the original plan's design has been improved by p1-foundation (typed Handle, `wf.node().out()`), prefer the newer API.

# What is already shipped (DO NOT REDO)

Verified by inspection on 2026-04-27:

- `vibecomfy/blocks/` — `__init__.py`, `_utils.py`, `decode.py`, `encoding.py`, `latent.py`, `loaders.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `subgraph.py`, `video.py`. The `Handles` wrapper exists and is typed against the new `Handle` dataclass per p1-foundation.
- `vibecomfy/patches/` — `builtins.py`, `controlnet.py`, `gguf_unet.py`, `ltx_lowvram.py`, `requirements.py`, `resolution.py`, `save_prefix.py`, `seed.py`, `registry.py`, `types.py`. Original T4 patch list (gguf_unet, ltx_lowvram, resolution, seed, save_prefix) plus extras.
- `VibeWorkflow.finalize_metadata()` — `vibecomfy/workflow.py:97`. Helpers in `vibecomfy/metadata.py`.
- `Handle` dataclass + `wf.node(class_type).out(slot)` typed authoring API — `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:16`, `vibecomfy/workflow.py:129`. Use these, not the older "string-only refs" assumption from the original plan.
- `tests/snapshots/` for 8 templates — pre-refactor API + class_type + widget_value snapshots already captured. Ready to drive snapshot-diff tests.
- `docs/authoring.md` (125 lines) — block + patch contracts, finalize_metadata, subgraph.opaque, "changes-handles → new template, decorates-handles → patch" rule. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 (ops/artifacts/router/recipes/plugin discovery) — see C5 below.**
- `AGENTS.md` at repo root. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 — see C5 below.**
- `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` exports `run_embedded`, `run_embedded_sync`, `smoke_runtime`, `smoke_runtime_sync` (T10 done).
- `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` includes the `untyped_raw_ref` lint rule from p1-foundation (separate from T17 patch suggestions, which is still missing).

## Layer A — Typed surface (was original T11–T13)

A1. **`vibecomfy/artifacts.py`** — frozen `Artifact` dataclass (`workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`. Methods: `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow`, `compile() -> dict`, `run(*, runtime='embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.{run_embedded_sync, run_sync}`.

   Note: original T10 listed this as "Depends on T1" and was marked done in `final.md`, but `vibecomfy/artifacts.py` does not exist on disk — verify and ship if missing. (Likely got rolled back when the plan aborted.)

A2. **`vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py` + `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`** — verb-native API returning typed Artifacts, lazy by default. Signatures from original T11:
   - `image.t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `image.edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `video.t2v(prompt, *, model=None, width=None, height=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `video.i2v(image, prompt, *, model=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `audio.t2a(...)` raises `NotImplementedError('no audio template registered')`
   - `edit.qwen(image, instruction, **overrides) -> Image` delegates to `image.edit`

   Op body: pick `(template_id, [patch, ...])` via the router (A3); load via `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)` (B1) or `workflow_from_template`; build VibeWorkflow; apply `set_prompt`/`set_seed` and patches; return typed artifact pointing at the workflow's terminal save node from `outputs`.

A3. **`vibecomfy/router.py` + `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`** — hand-written decision table per original T12. Inputs: `verb`, `inputs` (image presence, requested model, requested resolution/length), `environment` (declared model presence + GPU memory hint, both pluggable; for v1 stub `environment` to `{}` and document that callers can override — exhaustive GPU detection is MP-5 territory).

   **Rules must be exhaustive across all 9 refactored templates** (see C1) — every template must be reachable via at least one rule. The full required rule set covering the 9 templates:
   - `('image', 't2i', model in {'z_image', None}) → 'image/z_image'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf') → 'image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_t2v'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_t2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_i2v'`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_i2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('image', 'edit', model in {'qwen', None}) → 'edit/qwen_image_edit'`
   - `('image', 'edit', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled'`
   - Fall back to `KeyError('no template for verb=...')`. After template selection, call `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)` so router and doctor (C2) share the same probe. Data-driven rules in `router_rules.py` so plugins can extend via `register_route(verb, predicate, template_id, patches=())`.

A4. **Tests**: `tests/test_router.py` (rule table positive/negative + KeyError); `tests/test_ops.py` (`image.t2i('hello')` returns Image without executing; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` has SaveImage and a node carrying the prompt; `preview_workflow().inputs['prompt']` registered — proves finalize_metadata wires through ops). # Critical constraints (from original plan + new context)

- **VibeWorkflow remains the only editable IR.** Blocks must mutate via `add_node`/`connect`; patches must return VibeWorkflow; only `ingest/normalize.py` and `compile()` may edit API dicts directly.
- **Backward compatibility:** existing tests pass unchanged. Existing CLI flag surfaces stay identical. `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public-facing imports/CLI args must not diff.
- **MarkdownNote drop vs snapshot parity:** snapshots in `tests/snapshots/` were captured with MarkdownNote filtered at the source. Refactored templates drop MarkdownNote; snapshot test compares only runnable nodes. If you re-capture without filtering, parity test fails and refactor looks broken.
- **finalize_metadata parity is load-bearing:** `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` must show `inputs/outputs/requirements` equal between block-built workflow + `finalize_metadata()` and `convert_to_vibe_format()` for an equivalent graph. If parity breaks, `set_prompt`/`set_seed`/inspect/op-terminal-artifact-lookup all silently degrade.
- **ltx_lowvram external_python_marker parity:** new patch must produce the same marker key/value as the legacy `_ready_template_policy` mutator before that function is deleted. Verify with `tests/test_ready_templates.py:19` still green.
- **`vibecomfy/cli.py:23 _resolve_workflow_path`** must be either deleted or made to delegate to `load_workflow_any` — duplicate logic in two places will diverge.
- **Plugin discovery cold-start cost:** `_resolve_ready_path` calls `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily; cache empty-plugin result so `workflows list` does not slow on every invocation.
- **Built-in ready-template ids win on duplicate id with plugin templates;** collisions emit a warning, not an exception.
- **Lazy ops contract:** verbs return Artifact objects without executing; `Artifact.run()` triggers execution; `Artifact.preview_workflow()` returns the VibeWorkflow without running. Pipelines compose via file-handoff — single-workflow chaining is out of scope for v1.
- **`audio.t2a` is a `NotImplementedError` stub** until an audio ready template ships. Document in AGENTS.md and `docs/authoring.md`.
- **Each block/patch/op file should stay under ~250 lines** with a single responsibility.
- **`subgraph.opaque` is a passthrough wrapper for UUID-class subgraph nodes;** it cannot be decomposed without unpacking the ComfyUI subgraph definition. Each affected template names the output slot it actually wires (typically slot 0 → 'out').
- **Repo is NOT a git repo.** Do NOT run `git init`. Verify `.git/` does not exist after each phase.

# Out of scope (do NOT do)

- Re-doing anything in the "already shipped" list — verify it still works, but don't reimplement.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` external-mode handling / `ExternalServerRestartRequired` — deferred to MP-5 by p1-foundation.
- Block library expansion beyond what's needed for the 8-template refactor.
- VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration, `wrap_api_dict`, ExternalPythonNode, stage-aware run-dirs / `flow_id` / `stage_index` — MP-5 territory per the design doc.
- Schema-backed validation, `/object_info` snapshot, `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` — MP-6 territory.
- Compile-time serialization gate — MP-3 territory.
- RunPod end-to-end smoke as part of acceptance — `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` exists from p1-foundation but stays opt-in (`@pytest.mark.runpod`); do NOT execute during megaplan run.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Finish Higher-Abstraction Authoring Layer (v4)

## Overview

Layer 2 on top of the shipped Layer 1. Scope unchanged: typed `Artifact`, verb-native ops, hand-written `router`, plugin discovery (with op registry), layered loader (`resolve_workflow_path` returns `str`; `load_workflow_any` returns `VibeWorkflow`), `--json` output across the brief's 14 commands, doctor patch suggestions, refactor of the 9 snapshotted ready-templates, three example recipes, and the `[entry-points]` group declaration.

v4 closes 10 v3-surfaced flags via three concrete changes (no architectural redesign — root-cause flags assessment: none point to wrong code, all are local correctness gaps from inspecting actual snapshot/code state):

- **image.edit deferred to follow-up** (closes FLAG-006, issue_hints, correctness, callers-1, plus the still-listed correctness-2 + callers from v3's "warn + ignore" choice). The brief's `image.edit(image, instruction, ...)` signature cannot be honored against the only two snapshotted edit templates (qwen_image_edit and flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled both have no instruction widget per their snapshots — verified). Drop the two `('image', 'edit', ...)` router rules; keep `image.edit` and `edit.qwen` defined but raise `NotImplementedError("image.edit is deferred to a follow-up plan that re-authors edit/qwen_image_edit with an explicit instruction node — see docs/authoring.md")`. This mirrors the existing `audio.t2a` stub pattern (brief-sanctioned) and is honest about the constraint instead of silently ignoring `instruction`. The 9 snapshotted templates still all get refactored to block-based builders for snapshot parity — `qwen_image_edit` and `flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled` just aren't reachable via the verb-native API in this PR. This is a documented divergence from the brief listed in Question 1.
- **inspect.py raw `load_template` branch refactor** (closes FLAG-010, scope). Replace the `path = resolve_workflow_path(...) ; raw = load_template(path) ; shape = detect_workflow_shape(raw)` sequence with `wf = load_workflow_any(args.workflow); raw = wf.compile('api'); shape = detect_workflow_shape(raw)`. The shape probe runs on the compiled API dict (which is JSON-shaped, exactly what `detect_workflow_shape` expects), so it works uniformly for ready-id, .py, and .json inputs.
- **finalize_metadata output ordering** (closes FLAG-011, callers-2). Add a single deterministic sort to `finalize_metadata`: `self.outputs.sort(key=lambda o: (int(o.node_id) if o.node_id.isdigit() else (1<<30), o.node_id))`. Numeric node_ids sort first by integer value (LTX dual-SaveVideo case: 4823 then 4852 — picks the lower-numbered SaveVideo as `wf.outputs[0]`), non-numeric sort lexicographically after. Apply the same sort in `convert_to_vibe_format` so `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` parity holds. `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` extends to assert `outputs[0]` is the lower-numbered `SaveVideo` for a two-SaveVideo workflow.

All v3 settled decisions (SD-001 through SD-018) carry forward. v3's per-template prompt-field strategy for the seven non-deferred templates (z_image, flux2_klein_4b_t2i, flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i, wan_t2v, wan_i2v, ltx2_3_t2v, ltx2_3_i2v) is unchanged.

## Phase 1: Typed Surface (Layer A)

### Step 1: Add `vibecomfy/artifacts.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `Artifact` (frozen dataclass: `workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict = field(default_factory=dict)`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`.
2. **Methods:** `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow`; `compile() -> dict` (`self.workflow.compile('api')`); `run(*, runtime: str = 'embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded_sync`/`run_sync`; raise `ValueError` on unknown runtime.
3. **Cap** ~100 lines.

### Step 2: Op registry (`vibecomfy/ops/registry.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `_OPS: dict[tuple[str, str], Callable] = {}`, `register_op(verb_kind, verb_name, fn)`, `lookup_op(verb_kind, verb_name) -> Callable | None`.
2. **Built-ins** in Step 5 self-register at import.
3. **Dispatch** in `vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py`: `image`, `video`, `audio`, `edit` namespaces resolve `(verb_kind, verb_name)` against the registry; built-in calls submodule function; plugin overrides win with one-time `warnings.warn`.

### Step 3: Extend output registration + deterministic ordering (`vibecomfy/metadata.py`, `vibecomfy/workflow.py`, `vibecomfy/ingest/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `SaveVideo`, `SaveAudio`, `SaveAudioMP3` to `OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES` at `vibecomfy/metadata.py:13`. (`CreateVideo` is intentionally NOT added.)
2. **Add deterministic sort** to `VibeWorkflow.finalize_metadata()` after the output-collection loop:
   ```python
   self.outputs.sort(key=lambda o: (int(o.node_id) if o.node_id.isdigit() else (1<<30), o.node_id))
   ```
   Closes FLAG-011 / callers-2 by giving ops a stable `wf.outputs[0]` even with two `SaveVideo` branches (LTX case: ids 4823, 4852 → 4823 wins).
3. **Apply the same sort** in `vibecomfy/ingest/normalize.py:convert_to_vibe_format` (or wherever it builds the outputs list) so block-built and JSON-built workflows hash identically — required for `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` parity.
4. **Test** in Step 17 (`tests/test_finalize_metadata.py`) asserts: (a) `SaveVideo` registers as a `VibeOutput` after `finalize_metadata`; (b) for a workflow with two `SaveVideo` nodes ids `'10'` and `'5'`, `outputs[0].node_id == '5'`; (c) parity with `convert_to_vibe_format`.

### Step 4: Add the router (`vibecomfy/router.py`, `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`router_rules.py`**: list of `Rule(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches_factory)` plus `register_route(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches=())`. **Seed with seven rules only** — drop the two `('image', 'edit', ...)` rules from the brief because the underlying templates can't honor `image.edit(instruction=...)` (snapshot-confirmed: no instruction widget in either qwen_image_edit or flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled). LTX rules return `[ltx_lowvram_patch, resolution(384, 256, 9)]`.
2. **`router.py:pick(verb_kind, verb_name, *, workflow_loader=None, **inputs) -> RouterResult`** returning `RouterResult(template_id: str, explicit_patches: list[Patch], applicable_patches: list[Patch])`. Mandatory `find_applicable(workflow)` post-load. `applicable_patches` is the **gap** (still-applicable patches per the brief, expected `[]` for as-shipped templates that applied policy inline — consistent with doctor's 0-suggestion rule).
3. **Raise** `KeyError` on miss (including `('image', 'edit', ...)` since no rule remains).

### Step 5: Add `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **`image.py`** — `t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image`: call `router.pick`, load workflow, `wf.set_prompt(prompt)` if `'prompt' in wf.inputs`, `wf.set_seed(seed)` if not None, apply `result.explicit_patches`, apply `resolution(width, height).apply(wf)` only when caller passed non-default values, locate terminal `SaveImage` from `wf.outputs[0]`, return `Image(...)`.
   `edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`: **raise `NotImplementedError("image.edit is deferred to a follow-up plan that re-authors edit/qwen_image_edit with an explicit instruction node — see docs/authoring.md")`** (closes FLAG-006, issue_hints, correctness, callers-1).
2. **`video.py`** — `t2v` and `i2v`. Locate terminal `SaveVideo` from `wf.outputs[0]` (deterministic per Step 3 sort).
3. **`audio.py`** — `t2a(prompt, **kw)` raises `NotImplementedError("no audio template registered")`.
4. **`edit.py`** — `qwen(image, instruction, **overrides) -> Image` raises the same `NotImplementedError` as `image.edit` (delegates by raising directly, matching the deferred status).
5. **Self-register** each op via `vibecomfy.ops.registry.register_op(...)` at module bottom (including the two raising ops — registry shape stays consistent for plugin overrides).

### Step 6: Router + ops tests (`tests/test_router.py`, `tests/test_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`tests/test_router.py`** — one positive test for each of the **seven remaining rules** (`image.t2i` x3, `video.t2v` x2, `video.i2v` x2). Assertions: `result.template_id` and `result.explicit_patches` names match. Negative: `router.pick('image', 'edit')` raises `KeyError`. `router.pick('whatever', 'unknown')` raises `KeyError`. One assertion that `result.applicable_patches` is a `list[Patch]`; for the LTX rule, additionally `applicable_patches == []`.
2. **`tests/test_ops.py`** — `image.t2i('hello')` returns `Image` lazily; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` contains `SaveImage`; `wf.inputs['prompt']` is registered. `video.t2v('hello').preview_workflow().outputs[0].output_type == 'SaveVideo'` (proves Step 3). `image.edit(<dummy>, 'instruction')` raises `NotImplementedError` mentioning the deferred follow-up; `edit.qwen(<dummy>, 'instruction')` same. `audio.t2a('hello')` raises `NotImplementedError`.

## Phase 2: CLI Unification + Plugin Discovery (Layer B)

### Step 7: Layered loader (`vibecomfy/cli_loader.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Keep** `resolve_workflow_path(value: str) -> str` unchanged in signature; extend it to additionally accept ready-template ids (re-uses `_resolve_ready_path`).
2. **Create** `vibecomfy/cli_loader.py:load_workflow_any(path_or_id: str) -> VibeWorkflow` as the higher layer (ready-id → `workflow_from_ready`; else `path = resolve_workflow_path(value)`; dispatch on suffix).
3. **`tests/test_cli_loader.py`** covers ready-id, slash-id, scratchpad path, JSON path, missing-id `KeyError`/`FileNotFoundError`.

### Step 8: Refactor commands to use `load_workflow_any` — including inspect's raw branch (`vibecomfy/commands/{doctor,inspect,run,validate,convert}.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** workflow-construction in `doctor.py`, `run.py`, `validate.py`, `convert.py` with `load_workflow_any(args.path)`.
2. **`inspect.py` specifically** (closes FLAG-010, scope): replace `path = resolve_workflow_path(args.workflow); raw = load_template(path); shape = detect_workflow_shape(raw); workflow = load_workflow_reference(...)` with:
   ```python
   workflow = load_workflow_any(args.workflow)
   raw = workflow.compile('api')
   shape = detect_workflow_shape(raw)
   ```
   This works uniformly for ready-id (whose backing file is `.py`), `.py` scratchpads, and `.json` files. `detect_workflow_shape` operates on a JSON-shaped dict — `compile('api')` produces exactly that. No more JSON-decode-on-Python-source failure.
3. **Helpers** that still need a path string (`doctor._json_path_for_reference`, `convert.render_scratchpad`) keep calling `resolve_workflow_path` directly.
4. **Out of scope (documented):** `nodes install/ensure` and `fetch.py` keep their existing loaders.
5. **Run** `pytest tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py` after this step.

### Step 9: Plugin discovery + multi-root id resolution (`vibecomfy/extras.py`, `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/extras.py` with `PluginAPI`, `_LOADED`, `_API`, `load_plugins()`, `ensure_plugins_loaded()`. `PluginAPI` exposes `register_block`, `register_patch`, `register_op`, `register_route`, `register_ready_root`. Three discovery sources: project-local, user-global, entry points group `vibecomfy.plugins`.
2. **Refactor** `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`:
   - Replace `READY_ROOT` with `_ready_roots() -> list[Path]` (built-in + project-local + user-global + plugin-registered).
   - **Refactor `_template_id_for_path(path: Path) -> str`** to walk `_ready_roots()` and return `path.relative_to(matching_root).with_suffix('').as_posix()`. Raise `ValueError` if no root matches.
   - `ready_template_ids()` walks all roots; built-in wins on duplicate id with one-time `warnings.warn`.
   - Both `ready_template_ids()` and `_resolve_ready_path` call `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily.
3. **`tests/test_plugin_discovery.py`**: project-local fixture (tmp_path/vibecomfy_extras/...); user-global fixture (monkeypatched home); entry-point fixture (mock importlib.metadata). Each registers one plugin type. Assertions: discovery, dispatch (`vibecomfy.ops.image.<plugin_verb>` works), id-collision warning, `workflow_from_ready('plugin_id').metadata['ready_template'] == 'plugin_id'`.

### Step 10: `--json` output (`vibecomfy/commands/_output.py` + listed command modules)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/commands/_output.py:emit(payload, *, json: bool, text_renderer)`.
2. **Add `--json`** to `workflows list`, `nodes list`, `inspect`, `doctor`, `sources sync`, `analyze info`, `analyze diff`. For `analyze info|diff`: `--json` is an alias for `--format json`; `--format` wins on conflict.
3. **`inspect --json`** payload includes `"applicable_patches": [{"name", "rationale"}]` from `find_applicable`.
4. **`doctor --json`** payload includes `"suggested_patches": [...]` from the same.

### Step 11: Public exports + entry-point group (`vibecomfy/__init__.py`, `pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `vibecomfy/__init__.py` to additionally export: `Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any`. Note `image.edit` and `edit.qwen` are exported but raise `NotImplementedError` per Step 5 (matches the `audio.t2a` precedent — the namespace is present, the verb is deferred).
2. **Add** to `pyproject.toml`:
   ```toml
   [project.entry-points."vibecomfy.plugins"]
   # External packages register plugins here:
   #   my_plugin = "my_pkg:register"
   ```
3. **Smoke** `python -m vibecomfy.cli {sources,workflows,nodes,analyze,search,inspect,convert,validate,doctor,run,runtime,logs,runpod} --help` exits 0.

## Phase 4: Acceptance

### Step 18: Acceptance smoke (manual + automated)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap first**: `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_materialize_filtering.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x`.
2. **New tests**: `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x`.
3. **CLI regression**: `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py`.
4. **Full suite**: `pytest`.
5. **Spot-check doctor**: `python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py --json` (expect `suggested_patches: []`); hand-stripped variant (expect `ltx_lowvram` suggested).
6. **Plugin smoke**: drop `vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/smoke.py`; verify `python -c "from vibecomfy.registry.ready import workflow_from_ready; wf = workflow_from_ready('smoke'); print(wf.metadata['ready_template'])"` prints `smoke`; `python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list | grep smoke`; `python -m vibecomfy.cli inspect smoke` exits 0 (works because Step 8 routed inspect through `load_workflow_any` and derives `raw` from `wf.compile('api')`).
7. **Public-import smoke**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any"` exits 0.
8. **NotImplementedError smoke**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import image; image.edit('x.png', 'do something')"` exits non-zero with the deferred-follow-up message; `python -c "from vibecomfy import audio; audio.t2a('hi')"` same.
9. **Throwaway**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import image; print(list(image.t2i('a tiny smoke test').preview_workflow().compile('api').values())[-1])"` shows a `SaveImage` node.
10. **Confirm** `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public surfaces unchanged via file diff.

## Execution Order

1. Phase 1: Step 1 (artifacts) → Step 2 (op registry) → **Step 3 (output_node_names + finalize_metadata sort — load-bearing prerequisite for video ops)** → Step 4 (router, 7 rules) → Step 5 (ops, image.edit raises NotImplementedError) → Step 6 (tests).
2. Phase 2: Step 7 (loader) → Step 8 (command refactor incl. inspect raw-branch fix) → Step 9 (plugin discovery + multi-root id) → Step 10 (`--json`) → Step 11 (exports + entry-point).
3. Phase 3: Step 12 (doctor) → Step 13 (move helper) → Step 14 (template refactor, smallest first) → Step 15 (delete script + recipes) → Step 16 (tests) → Step 17 (docs).
4. Phase 4 last.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_materialize_filtering.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x` — fast; catches block/finalize/snapshot regressions, the moved `_finalise_model_assets` import, the `SaveVideo` output registration, and the dual-SaveVideo ordering.
2. `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x` — new contracts (router 7-rule shape, ops including image.edit raise, inspect-via-load_workflow_any, plugin id resolution).
3. `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py` — CLI refactor regression.
4. `pytest` — full suite.
5. CLI `--help` smoke for the brief's 14 subcommands.
6. Manual doctor + plugin smoke from Step 18 (using `inspect smoke` against a project-local fixture).
7. Confirm `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` and `@pytest.mark.runpod` tests are NOT executed.

  "questions": [
    "**image.edit deferral.** v4 defers `image.edit` and `edit.qwen` to a follow-up plan because the only two snapshotted edit templates (qwen_image_edit and flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled) expose no instruction widget per their snapshots. The brief lists image.edit in Layer A scope. Two paths: (a) ship as v4 plans \u2014 verb defined but raises NotImplementedError, follow-up plan re-authors edit templates with explicit instruction nodes (cleanest, mirrors audio.t2a precedent); (b) expand v4 scope to refactor edit/qwen_image_edit and edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled to include a TextEncodeQwenImageEdit (or equivalent) instruction node \u2014 this requires either re-capturing snapshots OR accepting non-parity for these two templates, both of which conflict with the snapshot-parity acceptance criterion. Confirm (a) is acceptable, or instruct to expand to (b).",
    "**finalize_metadata sort behavior change.** The new `outputs.sort` adds deterministic ordering to both `finalize_metadata` and `convert_to_vibe_format`. Existing tests that compare full workflow JSON byte-for-byte across paths might see ordering differences for workflows that previously had non-trivial output ordering. Plan applies the sort to both paths so parity holds, but if any external tooling relies on insertion-order outputs, this is a small behavior change. Confirm acceptable."
  ],

    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Opaque prompt mutability: inspected the snapshots for qwen_image_edit, flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled, and flux2_klein_4b_t2i. The v2 plan's register_input-on-subgraph strategy would either register a non-existent field or mutate the wrong node, so image.edit(..., instruction=...) and some t2i prompt overrides will not work as planned without a per-template prompt-field strategy.",

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package.",

# Why this exists

The original plan at `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan/plans/build-the-higher-abstraction-20260425-0953/final.md` was a full 20-task brief that landed about 4 tasks (T1, T2, T5, T10) before its execute step returned `partial` twice and the plan stalled at `state=aborted`. Subsequent work (`design-a-concise-architecture-20260426-1555` → `p1-foundation-typed-handle-20260426-1927`) shipped a typed `Handle` dataclass and `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs).out(...)` authoring API, but did NOT cover the higher-abstraction layer (ops, artifacts, router, recipes, plugin discovery). So the framework exists but the user-facing "ordinary Python pipelines" surface does not.

The original plan doc is the source of truth for design decisions and watch items — read it before planning. This brief deliberately does not restate task details that are still accurate there. Where the original plan's design has been improved by p1-foundation (typed Handle, `wf.node().out()`), prefer the newer API.

# What is already shipped (DO NOT REDO)

Verified by inspection on 2026-04-27:

- `vibecomfy/blocks/` — `__init__.py`, `_utils.py`, `decode.py`, `encoding.py`, `latent.py`, `loaders.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `subgraph.py`, `video.py`. The `Handles` wrapper exists and is typed against the new `Handle` dataclass per p1-foundation.
- `vibecomfy/patches/` — `builtins.py`, `controlnet.py`, `gguf_unet.py`, `ltx_lowvram.py`, `requirements.py`, `resolution.py`, `save_prefix.py`, `seed.py`, `registry.py`, `types.py`. Original T4 patch list (gguf_unet, ltx_lowvram, resolution, seed, save_prefix) plus extras.
- `VibeWorkflow.finalize_metadata()` — `vibecomfy/workflow.py:97`. Helpers in `vibecomfy/metadata.py`.
- `Handle` dataclass + `wf.node(class_type).out(slot)` typed authoring API — `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:16`, `vibecomfy/workflow.py:129`. Use these, not the older "string-only refs" assumption from the original plan.
- `tests/snapshots/` for 8 templates — pre-refactor API + class_type + widget_value snapshots already captured. Ready to drive snapshot-diff tests.
- `docs/authoring.md` (125 lines) — block + patch contracts, finalize_metadata, subgraph.opaque, "changes-handles → new template, decorates-handles → patch" rule. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 (ops/artifacts/router/recipes/plugin discovery) — see C5 below.**
- `AGENTS.md` at repo root. **Covers Layer 1 only — must be extended to cover Layer 2 — see C5 below.**
- `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` exports `run_embedded`, `run_embedded_sync`, `smoke_runtime`, `smoke_runtime_sync` (T10 done).
- `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` includes the `untyped_raw_ref` lint rule from p1-foundation (separate from T17 patch suggestions, which is still missing).

# What is still missing (THE SCOPE OF THIS PLAN)

Listed in dependency order. The original `final.md` task numbers are referenced for cross-checking; do not re-derive scope from scratch.

## Layer A — Typed surface (was original T11–T13)

A1. **`vibecomfy/artifacts.py`** — frozen `Artifact` dataclass (`workflow: VibeWorkflow`, `node_id: str`, `output_slot: int`, `kind: Literal['image','video','audio','latent','mask']`, `metadata: dict`) plus tag subclasses `Image`, `Video`, `Audio`, `Latent`, `Mask`. Methods: `preview_workflow() -> VibeWorkflow`, `compile() -> dict`, `run(*, runtime='embedded', **kwargs) -> RunResult` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.{run_embedded_sync, run_sync}`.

   Note: original T10 listed this as "Depends on T1" and was marked done in `final.md`, but `vibecomfy/artifacts.py` does not exist on disk — verify and ship if missing. (Likely got rolled back when the plan aborted.)

A2. **`vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py` + `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py`** — verb-native API returning typed Artifacts, lazy by default. Signatures from original T11:
   - `image.t2i(prompt, *, model=None, width=1024, height=1024, steps=None, seed=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `image.edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, **overrides) -> Image`
   - `video.t2v(prompt, *, model=None, width=None, height=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `video.i2v(image, prompt, *, model=None, length=None, fps=16, seed=None, **overrides) -> Video`
   - `audio.t2a(...)` raises `NotImplementedError('no audio template registered')`
   - `edit.qwen(image, instruction, **overrides) -> Image` delegates to `image.edit`

   Op body: pick `(template_id, [patch, ...])` via the router (A3); load via `cli_loader.load_workflow_any(template_id)` (B1) or `workflow_from_template`; build VibeWorkflow; apply `set_prompt`/`set_seed` and patches; return typed artifact pointing at the workflow's terminal save node from `outputs`.

A3. **`vibecomfy/router.py` + `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`** — hand-written decision table per original T12. Inputs: `verb`, `inputs` (image presence, requested model, requested resolution/length), `environment` (declared model presence + GPU memory hint, both pluggable; for v1 stub `environment` to `{}` and document that callers can override — exhaustive GPU detection is MP-5 territory).

   **Rules must be exhaustive across all 9 refactored templates** (see C1) — every template must be reachable via at least one rule. The full required rule set covering the 9 templates:
   - `('image', 't2i', model in {'z_image', None}) → 'image/z_image'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i'`
   - `('image', 't2i', model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf') → 'image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_t2v'`
   - `('video', 't2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_t2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model in {'wan', None}) → 'video/wan_i2v'`
   - `('video', 'i2v', model='ltx') → ('video/ltx2_3_i2v', [ltx_lowvram(), resolution(384, 256, 9)])`
   - `('image', 'edit', model in {'qwen', None}) → 'edit/qwen_image_edit'`
   - `('image', 'edit', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled'`
   - Fall back to `KeyError('no template for verb=...')`. After template selection, call `vibecomfy.patches.registry.find_applicable(workflow)` so router and doctor (C2) share the same probe. Data-driven rules in `router_rules.py` so plugins can extend via `register_route(verb, predicate, template_id, patches=())`.

A4. **Tests**: `tests/test_router.py` (rule table positive/negative + KeyError); `tests/test_ops.py` (`image.t2i('hello')` returns Image without executing; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` has SaveImage and a node carrying the prompt; `preview_workflow().inputs['prompt']` registered — proves finalize_metadata wires through ops). Skip end-to-end runs in CI.

## Layer B — CLI unification + plugin discovery (was original T14–T16, T19)

B1. **`vibecomfy/cli_loader.py:load_workflow_any(path_or_id) -> VibeWorkflow`** with dispatch order from original T14:

# Critical constraints (from original plan + new context)

- **VibeWorkflow remains the only editable IR.** Blocks must mutate via `add_node`/`connect`; patches must return VibeWorkflow; only `ingest/normalize.py` and `compile()` may edit API dicts directly.
- **Backward compatibility:** existing tests pass unchanged. Existing CLI flag surfaces stay identical. `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public-facing imports/CLI args must not diff.
- **MarkdownNote drop vs snapshot parity:** snapshots in `tests/snapshots/` were captured with MarkdownNote filtered at the source. Refactored templates drop MarkdownNote; snapshot test compares only runnable nodes. If you re-capture without filtering, parity test fails and refactor looks broken.
- **finalize_metadata parity is load-bearing:** `tests/test_finalize_metadata.py` must show `inputs/outputs/requirements` equal between block-built workflow + `finalize_metadata()` and `convert_to_vibe_format()` for an equivalent graph. If parity breaks, `set_prompt`/`set_seed`/inspect/op-terminal-artifact-lookup all silently degrade.
- **ltx_lowvram external_python_marker parity:** new patch must produce the same marker key/value as the legacy `_ready_template_policy` mutator before that function is deleted. Verify with `tests/test_ready_templates.py:19` still green.
- **`vibecomfy/cli.py:23 _resolve_workflow_path`** must be either deleted or made to delegate to `load_workflow_any` — duplicate logic in two places will diverge.
- **Plugin discovery cold-start cost:** `_resolve_ready_path` calls `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily; cache empty-plugin result so `workflows list` does not slow on every invocation.
- **Built-in ready-template ids win on duplicate id with plugin templates;** collisions emit a warning, not an exception.
- **Lazy ops contract:** verbs return Artifact objects without executing; `Artifact.run()` triggers execution; `Artifact.preview_workflow()` returns the VibeWorkflow without running. Pipelines compose via file-handoff — single-workflow chaining is out of scope for v1.
- **`audio.t2a` is a `NotImplementedError` stub** until an audio ready template ships. Document in AGENTS.md and `docs/authoring.md`.
- **Each block/patch/op file should stay under ~250 lines** with a single responsibility.
- **`subgraph.opaque` is a passthrough wrapper for UUID-class subgraph nodes;** it cannot be decomposed without unpacking the ComfyUI subgraph definition. Each affected template names the output slot it actually wires (typically slot 0 → 'out').
- **Repo is NOT a git repo.** Do NOT run `git init`. Verify `.git/` does not exist after each phase.

# Out of scope (do NOT do)

- Re-doing anything in the "already shipped" list — verify it still works, but don't reimplement.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` external-mode handling / `ExternalServerRestartRequired` — deferred to MP-5 by p1-foundation.
- Block library expansion beyond what's needed for the 8-template refactor.
- VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration, `wrap_api_dict`, ExternalPythonNode, stage-aware run-dirs / `flow_id` / `stage_index` — MP-5 territory per the design doc.
- Schema-backed validation, `/object_info` snapshot, `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` — MP-6 territory.
- Compile-time serialization gate — MP-3 territory.
- RunPod end-to-end smoke as part of acceptance — `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` exists from p1-foundation but stays opt-in (`@pytest.mark.runpod`); do NOT execute during megaplan run.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Finish Higher-Abstraction Authoring Layer (v5)

## Overview

Layer 2 on top of the shipped Layer 1. v5 reverses v4's verb-deferral approach because the gate (and the brief) require verb-native `image.edit` and a 9-rule router. The structural tension is real — three snapshotted templates (qwen_image_edit, flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled, flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i) have no editable prompt/instruction widget in their captured snapshots — but the resolution that honors **both** the brief's verb contract **and** snapshot parity is to do the prompt/instruction node insertion **at the op layer, not the builder layer**:

- **Builders** preserve snapshot parity exactly (no extra nodes).
- **Ops** detect when a routed template lacks a prompt-bearing node and add a `TextEncodeQwenImageEdit` (qwen edit), `PrimitiveStringMultiline` (flux templates), or equivalent node, then call `set_prompt`. The added node is wired into the UUID subgraph's `text` input slot (matching the pattern in `flux2_klein_4b_t2i.api.json` where node 76 PrimitiveStringMultiline → subgraph `text` input).
- The **builder→op separation** keeps `tests/test_ready_templates.py` snapshot diff green (builder output unchanged) while ops produce a workflow that can honor the caller's prompt/instruction.

This is one explicit assumption the user must validate (Question 1): the subgraph input slot name `text` is inferred from `flux2_klein_4b_t2i`'s wiring — if the qwen subgraph doesn't expose a `text` input, MP-6 schema introspection is needed and the op falls back to a clear `RuntimeError("subgraph does not expose a text input — see MP-6")`.

v5 closes 14 v4-surfaced flags via four concrete changes:

- **Verb-contract restoration** (closes FLAG-006, issue_hints, issue_hints-1, FLAG-013, all_locations, callers, correctness, FLAG-012). Restore the two `('image', 'edit', ...)` router rules — router goes back to 9 rules. Implement `image.edit` and `edit.qwen` for real. For routes where the underlying template has no prompt-bearing node (qwen_image_edit, flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled, flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i), the op adds the node + edge before calling `set_prompt`.
- **convert.py ready-id regression fix** (closes scope, FLAG-014). Don't extend `resolve_workflow_path` to handle ready-template ids. Have `load_workflow_any` do ready-id resolution itself before delegating to `resolve_workflow_path` for path-only inputs. `convert.py` adds an explicit ready-id branch: if the input is a ready-id, route through `load_workflow_any` and write the compiled API JSON as the scratchpad source — never hands a `.py` path to `workflow_from_file`.
- **`run --ready` acceptance smoke restored** (closes FLAG-015, issue_hints-2). Add `python -m vibecomfy.cli run smoke --ready --runtime embedded` to acceptance Step 18 (using a fixture template under `vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/smoke.py` that's a minimal SaveImage workflow with no model dependencies — exits during embedded execution but proves the run-path resolution works end-to-end). Plus the `inspect smoke` and `workflows list | grep smoke` smokes from v4.
- **Carry-over from v3/v4 (unchanged):** `OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES` adds SaveVideo/SaveAudio*; `finalize_metadata` deterministic output sort; layered loader; plugin discovery + `_template_id_for_path` walks roots; doctor patch suggestions; 9-template builder refactor for snapshot parity; recipes; helpers (`register_input`, `apply_authored_metadata`); `_finalise_model_assets` migration.

All v4 settled decisions (SD-001..SD-020) carry forward except SD-013-implicit "router is 7 rules" which v5 reverts to 9.

## Phase 1: Typed Surface (Layer A)

### Step 1: Add `vibecomfy/artifacts.py`
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `Artifact` (frozen dataclass) plus tag subclasses `Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask`.
2. **Methods:** `preview_workflow()`, `compile()`, `run(*, runtime='embedded', **kwargs)` dispatching to `vibecomfy.runtime.run_embedded_sync`/`run_sync`; `ValueError` on unknown runtime.

### Step 2: Op registry (`vibecomfy/ops/registry.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `_OPS: dict[tuple[str, str], Callable]`, `register_op(verb_kind, verb_name, fn)`, `lookup_op(verb_kind, verb_name)`.
2. **Built-ins** in Step 5 self-register at import.
3. **Dispatch** in `vibecomfy/ops/__init__.py`: `image, video, audio, edit` namespaces resolve `(verb_kind, verb_name)` against the registry; plugin overrides win with one-time warn.

### Step 3: Extend output registration + deterministic ordering (`vibecomfy/metadata.py`, `vibecomfy/workflow.py`, `vibecomfy/ingest/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `SaveVideo`, `SaveAudio`, `SaveAudioMP3` to `OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES` at `vibecomfy/metadata.py:13`.
2. **Add deterministic sort** to `finalize_metadata()` after the output-collection loop:
   ```python
   self.outputs.sort(key=lambda o: (int(o.node_id) if o.node_id.isdigit() else (1<<30), o.node_id))
   ```
3. **Apply the same sort** in `convert_to_vibe_format` so block-built and JSON-built workflows hash identically.
4. **Test in Step 17** asserts SaveVideo registers + dual-SaveVideo deterministic ordering + parity with `convert_to_vibe_format`.

### Step 4: Add the router with all 9 rules (`vibecomfy/router.py`, `vibecomfy/router_rules.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`router_rules.py`**: list of `Rule(verb_kind, verb_name, predicate, template_id, patches_factory)` plus `register_route(...)`. **Seed with all 9 brief rules** including `('image', 'edit', model in {'qwen', None}) → 'edit/qwen_image_edit'` and `('image', 'edit', model='flux2_klein_4b') → 'edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled'`. LTX rules return `[ltx_lowvram_patch, resolution(384, 256, 9)]`.
2. **`router.py:pick(verb_kind, verb_name, *, workflow_loader=None, **inputs) -> RouterResult`** returning `RouterResult(template_id, explicit_patches, applicable_patches)`. Mandatory `find_applicable(workflow)` post-load. `applicable_patches` is the gap (expected `[]` for as-shipped templates with policy applied inline).
3. **Raise** `KeyError` only on truly unknown verbs.

### Step 5: Add `vibecomfy/ops/{image,video,audio,edit}.py` with prompt/instruction backfill
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** a small `vibecomfy/ops/_backfill.py` helper:
   ```python
   def ensure_prompt_input(wf: VibeWorkflow, *, instruction_class: str = 'PrimitiveStringMultiline',
                          subgraph_input: str = 'text') -> None:
       """If wf.inputs lacks 'prompt', find the terminal subgraph and inject an instruction node.
       For qwen subgraphs use class='TextEncodeQwenImageEdit' (in PROMPT_NODE_CLASSES so finalize_metadata auto-registers).
       For flux subgraphs use class='PrimitiveStringMultiline' wired into the subgraph's `text` input,
       then call wf.register_input('prompt', primitive.id, 'widget_0', placeholder).
       Raise RuntimeError if no UUID-class subgraph node found, or if the subgraph already has a wired
       'text' input (the op should respect the existing wiring)."""
   ```
2. **`image.py`** — `t2i(prompt, *, model=None, ...)`: route, load workflow, `ensure_prompt_input(wf)` if `'prompt' not in wf.inputs`, `wf.set_prompt(prompt)`, `wf.set_seed(seed)`, apply patches, locate `SaveImage` from `wf.outputs[0]`, return `Image`.
   `edit(image, instruction, *, model=None, ...)`: route, load workflow, write `image` to `LoadImage` widget if present (else `wf.add_node('LoadImage', widget_0=image)` and wire), `ensure_prompt_input(wf, instruction_class='TextEncodeQwenImageEdit' if model in {'qwen', None} else 'PrimitiveStringMultiline')`, `wf.set_prompt(instruction)`, return `Image`.
3. **`video.py`** — `t2v` and `i2v`. Same pattern. Locate `SaveVideo` from `wf.outputs[0]` (deterministic per Step 3).
4. **`audio.py`** — `t2a(prompt, **kw)` raises `NotImplementedError("no audio template registered")` (brief-sanctioned).
5. **`edit.py`** — `qwen(image, instruction, **overrides)` calls `image.edit(image, instruction, model='qwen', **overrides)`.
6. **Self-register** each op via `vibecomfy.ops.registry.register_op(...)` at module bottom.

### Step 6: Router + ops tests (`tests/test_router.py`, `tests/test_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`tests/test_router.py`** — one positive test for each of the **9 rules** (`image.t2i` ×3, `video.t2v` ×2, `video.i2v` ×2, `image.edit` ×2). Assertions: `result.template_id` and `result.explicit_patches` names. Negative: `router.pick('whatever', 'unknown')` raises `KeyError`. `result.applicable_patches` is `list[Patch]`; for LTX rule additionally `applicable_patches == []`.
2. **`tests/test_ops.py`** — `image.t2i('hello')` returns `Image`; `Image.preview_workflow().compile('api')` contains `SaveImage`; `wf.inputs['prompt']` registered. `video.t2v('hello').preview_workflow().outputs[0].output_type == 'SaveVideo'`. **`image.edit(<dummy>, 'instruction')` returns `Image`; the resulting `compile('api')` contains a `TextEncodeQwenImageEdit` node carrying the instruction text** (proves backfill). For flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i: `image.t2i('hi', model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf').preview_workflow().compile('api')` contains a `PrimitiveStringMultiline` node carrying 'hi'. `audio.t2a('hi')` raises `NotImplementedError`.

---

## Phase 2: CLI Unification + Plugin Discovery (Layer B)

### Step 7: Layered loader without modifying resolve_workflow_path's contract (`vibecomfy/cli_loader.py`, `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Keep** `vibecomfy/commands/_workflow_path.py:resolve_workflow_path(value: str) -> str` **unchanged in both signature and behavior** (JSON-index lookup + path passthrough). Do **not** extend it to accept ready-template ids — that's what broke convert.py per FLAG-014.
2. **Create** `vibecomfy/cli_loader.py:load_workflow_any(path_or_id: str) -> VibeWorkflow` as the higher layer that does ready-id resolution itself:
   - If `value in ready_template_ids()` → `workflow_from_ready(value)`.
   - Else `path = resolve_workflow_path(value)`; dispatch on suffix: `.py` → `load_scratchpad(path)`; `.json` → `convert_to_vibe_format(normalize_to_api(load_template(path), ...), source_path=path, ...)`.
3. **`tests/test_cli_loader.py`** covers ready-id, slash-id, scratchpad path, JSON path, missing-id `KeyError` / `FileNotFoundError`.

### Step 8: Refactor commands to use `load_workflow_any` (`vibecomfy/commands/{doctor,inspect,run,validate,convert}.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** workflow-construction in `doctor.py`, `run.py`, `validate.py` with `load_workflow_any(args.path)`.
2. **`inspect.py`** (closes FLAG-010 from v3): replace the `path = resolve_workflow_path(args.workflow); raw = load_template(path); shape = detect_workflow_shape(raw); workflow = load_workflow_reference(...)` block with:
   ```python
   workflow = load_workflow_any(args.workflow)
   raw = workflow.compile('api')
   shape = detect_workflow_shape(raw)
   ```
3. **`convert.py`** (closes FLAG-014): add an explicit ready-id branch at the top:
   ```python
   if args.workflow in ready_template_ids():
       workflow = workflow_from_ready(args.workflow)
       api = workflow.compile('api')
       # Render scratchpad from the compiled API dict (existing scratchpad rendering accepts JSON-shaped dicts via source_is_path=False with the dict serialized to a temp file, OR we add a render_scratchpad_from_dict helper).
       text = render_scratchpad_from_dict(api, schema_provider=...)
       Path(args.out).write_text(text, encoding='utf-8')
       return 0
   # else: existing path-based flow unchanged
   ```
   Add the small helper `vibecomfy/scratchpad_loader.py:render_scratchpad_from_dict(api_dict, *, schema_provider)` if one doesn't exist (extract from the existing `render_scratchpad` JSON-path branch). This avoids ever passing a `.py` ready-template path to `workflow_from_file` (which is JSON-only).
4. **Helpers** that still need a path string (`doctor._json_path_for_reference`, `convert.render_scratchpad` non-ready-id branch) keep calling `resolve_workflow_path` directly.
5. **Out of scope (documented):** `nodes install/ensure` and `fetch.py` keep their existing loaders.
6. **Run** `pytest tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py` after this step.

### Step 9: Plugin discovery + multi-root id resolution (`vibecomfy/extras.py`, `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/extras.py` with `PluginAPI` (register_block, register_patch, register_op, register_route, register_ready_root), `_LOADED`, `_API`, `load_plugins()`, `ensure_plugins_loaded()`. Three sources: project-local, user-global, entry-points group `vibecomfy.plugins`.
2. **Refactor** `vibecomfy/registry/ready.py`:
   - Replace `READY_ROOT` with `_ready_roots() -> list[Path]` (built-in + project-local + user-global + plugin-registered).
   - **Refactor `_template_id_for_path(path)`** to walk `_ready_roots()` and return id relative to matching root.
   - `ready_template_ids()` walks all roots; built-in wins on duplicate id with one-time warn.
   - Both `ready_template_ids()` and `_resolve_ready_path` call `ensure_plugins_loaded()` lazily.
3. **`tests/test_plugin_discovery.py`**: project-local fixture, user-global fixture (monkeypatched home), entry-point fixture. Each registers a plugin type. Assertions: discovery, `vibecomfy.ops.image.<plugin_verb>` dispatch, id-collision warning, `workflow_from_ready('plugin_id').metadata['ready_template'] == 'plugin_id'`.

### Step 10: `--json` output (`vibecomfy/commands/_output.py` + listed command modules)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/commands/_output.py:emit(payload, *, json: bool, text_renderer)`.
2. **Add `--json`** to `workflows list`, `nodes list`, `inspect`, `doctor`, `sources sync`, `analyze info`, `analyze diff`. For `analyze info|diff`: `--json` is an alias for `--format json`; `--format` wins on conflict.
3. **`inspect --json`** payload includes `"applicable_patches": [{"name", "rationale"}]`.
4. **`doctor --json`** payload includes `"suggested_patches": [...]`.

### Step 11: Public exports + entry-point group (`vibecomfy/__init__.py`, `pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `vibecomfy/__init__.py` to export: `Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any`.
2. **Add** to `pyproject.toml`:
   ```toml
   [project.entry-points."vibecomfy.plugins"]
   # External packages register plugins here:
   #   my_plugin = "my_pkg:register"
   ```
3. **Smoke** `python -m vibecomfy.cli {sources,workflows,nodes,analyze,search,inspect,convert,validate,doctor,run,runtime,logs,runpod} --help` exits 0.

**Scope:** Small
1. **Move** `_finalise_model_assets` and helpers from `scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py:570-613` to `vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py:finalise_model_assets`.
2. **Update** `tests/test_materialize_filtering.py` import.
3. **Run** `pytest tests/test_materialize_filtering.py -x`.

### Step 14: Add helpers + refactor 9 ready_templates (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`, `vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py`, `ready_templates/{video,image,edit}/<id>.py`)
**Scope:** Large

**Helpers first:**
1. **Add** `VibeWorkflow.register_input(name, node_id, field, value)`.
2. **Add** `apply_authored_metadata(workflow, ready_metadata, *, requirements, source_path)` to `vibecomfy/registry/ready_template.py`.

**Per-template construction strategy** — all 9 templates rebuilt as block-based builders that **preserve snapshot parity exactly** (no extra prompt nodes added at builder time). Prompt registration happens only when the snapshot already has a prompt-bearing node:

| Template | Builder approach | Prompt registration in builder |
|---|---|---|
| `image/z_image` | UUID subgraph + SaveImage | `wf.register_input('prompt', subgraph.id, 'widget_0', PROMPT)` (subgraph widget_0 carries text) |
| `image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i` | PrimitiveStringMultiline + 2× UUID subgraph + 2× SaveImage | `wf.register_input('prompt', primitive.id, 'widget_0', PROMPT)` |
| `image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i` | UUID subgraph (empty inputs) + SaveImage | **None at builder time** — snapshot has no prompt node. Op layer (Step 5) adds PrimitiveStringMultiline + wires to subgraph `text` input when called via `image.t2i(model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf')`. |
| `edit/qwen_image_edit` | UUID subgraph + LoadImage + ImageScaleToTotalPixels + SaveImage | **None at builder time**. Op layer adds TextEncodeQwenImageEdit + wires to subgraph `text` input when called via `image.edit(model='qwen')`. |
| `edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled` | UUID subgraph(s) + LoadImage + SaveImage | **None at builder time**. Op layer adds PrimitiveStringMultiline (or TextEncodeQwenImageEdit if applicable) + wires to subgraph `text` input. |
| `video/wan_t2v` | 10 named class types via `wf.node()` | `CLIPTextEncode` auto-registers via PROMPT_NODE_CLASSES |
| `video/wan_i2v` | Similar | `CLIPTextEncode` auto-registers |
| `video/ltx2_3_t2v` | 33 named class types via `wf.node()`; apply ltx_lowvram + resolution(384,256,9) inline | `CLIPTextEncode` and/or `GemmaAPITextEncode` (sub-step 14a) |
| `video/ltx2_3_i2v` | Similar | Same |

**Sub-step 14a**: At LTX refactor time, decide whether `GemmaAPITextEncode` goes into `vibecomfy/metadata.py:PROMPT_NODE_CLASSES`.

**Per-template construction order (smallest first; run snapshot test after each):**
1. `image/z_image.py`
2. `image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py`
3. `image/flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i.py` (no prompt at builder; ops backfill)
4. `edit/qwen_image_edit.py` (no prompt at builder; ops backfill)
5. `edit/flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled.py` (no prompt at builder; ops backfill)
6. `video/wan_t2v.py`
7. `video/wan_i2v.py`
8. `video/ltx2_3_t2v.py`
9. `video/ltx2_3_i2v.py`

**Snapshot diff test** in `tests/test_ready_templates.py`: parametrized over 9 ids. Comparison: `frozenset((class_type, frozenset(widget_value_items)))` plus topology hash. MarkdownNote stripped. **Critical:** the snapshot diff runs against the BUILDER output (`workflow_from_ready(id).compile('api')`), NOT the op-mutated output. This preserves snapshot parity for the 9 templates while letting ops mutate at call time.

**Op-side backfill test** (in `tests/test_ops.py` per Step 6): for each route whose builder doesn't register a prompt, calling `image.t2i(prompt, model=...)` or `image.edit(image, instruction, model=...)` produces a workflow whose `compile('api')` contains the backfill node carrying the caller's text.

**Scope:** Small

**Scope:** Small

2. **Extend** `tests/test_patches.py` with `applies_to` positive + negative + `apply` diff.

### Step 17: Documentation extension (`docs/authoring.md`, `AGENTS.md`)

## Phase 4: Acceptance

### Step 18: Acceptance smoke (manual + automated)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap first**: `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_materialize_filtering.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x`.
2. **New tests**: `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x`.
3. **CLI regression**: `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py`.
4. **Full suite**: `pytest`.
5. **Spot-check doctor**: `python -m vibecomfy.cli doctor ready_templates/video/ltx2_3_t2v.py --json` (`suggested_patches: []`); hand-stripped variant (`ltx_lowvram` suggested).
6. **Plugin smoke (covers brief's full requirement — closes FLAG-015)**: drop `vibecomfy_extras/ready_templates/smoke.py` (a minimal `wf.add_node('SaveImage', images=['1', 0]).out(0)` workflow with one upstream EmptyImage-equivalent — no model deps so embedded execution exits cleanly). Verify all four resolution paths from the brief:
   - `python -c "from vibecomfy.registry.ready import workflow_from_ready; print(workflow_from_ready('smoke').metadata['ready_template'])"` prints `smoke`
   - `python -c "from vibecomfy.registry.library import workflow_from_template; workflow_from_template('smoke')"` exits 0
   - `python -m vibecomfy.cli workflows list | grep smoke`
   - `python -m vibecomfy.cli inspect smoke` exits 0
   - **`python -m vibecomfy.cli run smoke --ready --runtime embedded`** exits 0 (or with a runtime error that's NOT a path-resolution failure — log message must show the workflow loaded successfully before any execution failure)
7. **Public-import smoke**: `python -c "from vibecomfy import Artifact, Image, Video, Audio, Latent, Mask, image, video, audio, edit, blocks, patches, router, ensure_plugins_loaded, load_workflow_any"` exits 0.
8. **Verb smoke**: `image.t2i('hi').preview_workflow().compile('api')` shows SaveImage; `image.edit('img.png', 'make it red')` produces a workflow with `TextEncodeQwenImageEdit` carrying `'make it red'`; `audio.t2a('hi')` raises `NotImplementedError`.
9. **Convert ready-id smoke (closes FLAG-014)**: `python -m vibecomfy.cli convert smoke --out /tmp/smoke_scratchpad.py` produces a Python scratchpad without the `workflow_from_file` JSON-decode error.
10. **Confirm** `scripts/runpod_runner.py` and `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py` public surfaces unchanged via file diff.

## Execution Order

1. Phase 1: Step 1 → Step 2 → **Step 3 (output_node_names + sort)** → Step 4 (router, all 9 rules) → Step 5 (ops with backfill helper) → Step 6 (tests).
2. Phase 2: Step 7 (loader — does NOT extend `resolve_workflow_path`) → Step 8 (command refactor, including convert ready-id branch + inspect raw fix) → Step 9 (plugin discovery) → Step 10 (`--json`) → Step 11 (exports + entry-point).
3. Phase 3: Step 12 → Step 13 → Step 14 (template refactor preserves snapshot parity, ops backfill at call time) → Step 15 → Step 16 → Step 17.
4. Phase 4 last.

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_workflow_core.py tests/test_finalize_metadata.py tests/test_materialize_filtering.py tests/test_ready_templates.py -x` — fast; catches block/finalize/snapshot regressions.
2. `pytest tests/test_router.py tests/test_ops.py tests/test_cli_loader.py tests/test_plugin_discovery.py -x` — new contracts (9-rule router, image.edit backfill, plugin id resolution).
3. `pytest tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py tests/test_doctor_models.py tests/test_cli.py tests/test_run_command.py` — CLI regression including convert ready-id and inspect refactor.
4. `pytest` — full suite.
5. CLI `--help` smoke for the brief's 14 subcommands.
6. Manual smokes from Step 18 — including `run smoke --ready --runtime embedded` and `convert smoke --out ...`.
7. Confirm RunPod tests unexecuted.
