  "assumptions": [
    "Leave `.git/` in place.",
    "`--help` and `--json` rollout target the brief's 14 commands.",
    "`scripts/materialize_ready_templates.py` deletion is acceptable after `_finalise_model_assets` is moved.",
    "Refactored builders set `metadata['python_policy_applied'] = True` via apply_authored_metadata.",
    "`router.pick` returns `RouterResult(template_id, explicit_patches, applicable_patches)` with applicable_patches as the gap; expected to be `[]` for as-shipped LTX.",
    "Op signatures `steps`, `fps`, `width`, `height`, `length` map to widget overrides or the parametric resolution patch; no new patch class.",
    "LTX builders apply `ltx_lowvram.apply(wf)` and `resolution(384, 256, 9).apply(wf)` inline.",
    "`Artifact` subclasses are tag types fixing the `kind` literal.",
    "`[project.entry-points.\"vibecomfy.plugins\"]` declared with comment-only example.",
    "Recipes live at vibecomfy repo root.",
    "RunPod smoke stays opt-in.",
    "Builders/patches may use any `VibeWorkflow` method.",
    "`SaveVideo`, `SaveAudio`, `SaveAudioMP3` added to `OUTPUT_NODE_NAMES`.",
    "`finalize_metadata` and `convert_to_vibe_format` both sort `outputs` by node_id.",
    "**Op-side prompt/instruction backfill**: when a routed template's builder does not register a 'prompt' input (qwen_image_edit, flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled, flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i), the op layer (vibecomfy/ops/_backfill.py) injects a prompt-bearing node (TextEncodeQwenImageEdit for qwen route, PrimitiveStringMultiline for flux routes) and wires it into the UUID subgraph's `text` input slot. The subgraph's input slot name `text` is inferred from `flux2_klein_4b_t2i.api.json`'s wiring pattern (PrimitiveStringMultiline node 76 wires to subgraph 75 via `text`). If a UUID subgraph rejects this wiring at runtime, the op raises `RuntimeError(\"subgraph does not expose 'text' input \u2014 MP-6 schema introspection needed\")`. Flagged as Question 1 for user validation.",
    "**Snapshot parity is preserved at the BUILDER level**, not the op level. `tests/test_ready_templates.py` snapshot diff runs against `workflow_from_ready(id).compile('api')` (builder output, no op mutation). Op-mutated workflows (from `image.t2i`, `image.edit`, etc.) are tested separately in `tests/test_ops.py` and are NOT compared to snapshots.",
    "**`resolve_workflow_path` is NOT extended in v5** \u2014 it stays a pure path/index resolver returning `str`. The ready-id resolution lives in `load_workflow_any` (the higher layer). This reverts v4's extension which broke `convert.py`'s `workflow_from_file` path.",
    "**`convert.py` adds an explicit ready-id branch** that compiles via `workflow_from_ready` then writes the API JSON as a scratchpad source via a new `render_scratchpad_from_dict` helper extracted from the existing `render_scratchpad`'s JSON-path branch. No .py paths reach `workflow_from_file`.",
    "**`run --ready` acceptance smoke** uses a fixture template with no model dependencies (e.g., bare `SaveImage` chain) so embedded execution can complete without GPU. Acceptable to allow runtime-internal failures provided the run-path resolution succeeds (verified by log message ordering or by checking that the failure is NOT `FileNotFoundError`/`KeyError`).",
    "Per-template prompt-field strategy (4 of 9 templates) unchanged from v3: z_image, flux2_klein_4b_t2i, wan_t2v, wan_i2v register prompt at builder time. The other 5 (flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i, qwen_image_edit, flux2_klein_4b_image_edit_distilled, ltx2_3_t2v, ltx2_3_i2v) either auto-register via PROMPT_NODE_CLASSES (CLIPTextEncode/GemmaAPITextEncode for ltx) or rely on op-side backfill (the three with empty/missing prompt nodes)."
  ],
  "delta_from_previous_percent": 65.11,
  "structure_warnings": []
}

    "id": "issue_hints-1",

    "severity": "significant"

The two edit snapshots expose no prompt/instruction widget at all (only LoadImage, SaveImage, and UUID subgraphs), while flux2_klein_4b_t2i carries the prompt in a PrimitiveStringMultiline node wired into two UUID subgraphs, not on the subgraph itself. The v2 plan's register_input-on-subgraph strategy would either register a non-existent field or mutate the wrong node, so image.edit(..., instruction=...) and some t2i prompt overrides will not work as planned without a per-template prompt-field strategy.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "callers",
    "concern": "Find the callers of the changed function. What arguments do they actually pass? Does the fix handle all of them?: set_prompt callers: v4 removes set_prompt calls for edit templates by deferring image.edit, which avoids calling set_input against non-existent instruction fields. However, the remaining flux2_klein_9b_gguf_t2i t2i route still has no visible prompt-bearing field in its snapshot, so `image.t2i(prompt, model='flux2_klein_9b_gguf')` will either ignore the prompt or require a non-parity graph change.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },

    "id": "FLAG-006",
    "concern": "Edit API deferral: v4 replaces the broken image.edit implementation with NotImplementedError, but this still does not satisfy the brief's requested image.edit/edit.qwen verb-native API or the required image/edit router rules.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "issue_hints",
    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: Verb-native edit contract: compared the brief's `image.edit(image, instruction, ...)` requirement with v3's qwen/flux edit behavior. V3 documents both snapshotted edit templates as image-only and says `instruction=` is ignored with a warning, which is an explicit divergence from the requested instruction-driven edit API rather than a full implementation of that verb.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "scope",
    "concern": "Search for related code that handles the same concept. Is the reported issue a symptom of something broader?: Convert command interaction: checked current convert.py and scratchpad_loader.render_scratchpad. V4 extends resolve_workflow_path so ready-template ids resolve to `.py` files, while convert keeps using resolve_workflow_path and sets `source_is_path=True`; render_scratchpad then emits `workflow_from_file('/.../ready_templates/...py')`, but workflow_from_file expects JSON and will fail on a Python ready template. This is a new regression risk for converting ready-template ids.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-010",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan places _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ but also needs vibeworkflow.connect in vibecomfy/workflow.py to consult handle_trace_active and record events. because importing vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log executes vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py, and that package currently imports vibeworkflow at top level, any top-level import from workflow.py to vibecomfy.blocks._coercion_log would create a circular import risk unless the implementation uses carefully lazy imports or moves the shared trace module outside the blocks package.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "mp-2-implement-p1-hard-gates-20260426-1845"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-006",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the proposed _coercion_log.py under vibecomfy/blocks/ is supporting infrastructure that both blocks and workflow need. given the current package dependency direction (blocks imports workflow), the safer location is a neutral module such as vibecomfy/handles.py or vibecomfy/handle_warnings.py; leaving it under blocks increases import-order fragility unless the implementation is very deliberate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "mp-2-implement-p1-hard-gates-20260426-1845"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: for the block helper caller path, vibeworkflow.connect is usually called by vibecomfy.blocks._utils.connect, not by the user's block function directly. the plan's immediate-caller-frame heuristic can work if _utils.connect has a handle local named source, but it will not identify the higher-level block function unless record_raw_ref_coercion preserves or walks to the block frame rather than skipping blocks frames.",
